180: async def _load_boxes(
181:     session: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, warehouse_id: uuid.UUID
182: ) -> tuple[list[WarehouseBox], list[InboundIntakeBox], list[InboundIntakeCargoPlace]]:
183:     warehouse_boxes = list(
184:         (
185:             await session.scalars(
186:                 select(WarehouseBox).where(
187:                     WarehouseBox.tenant_id == tenant_id,
188:                     WarehouseBox.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
189:                 )
190:             )
191:         ).all()
192:     )
193:     inbound_boxes = list(
194:         (
195:             await session.scalars(
196:                 select(InboundIntakeBox)
197:                 .join(InboundIntakeRequest)
198:                 .outerjoin(
199:                     StorageLocation,
200:                     StorageLocation.id == InboundIntakeBox.storage_location_id,
201:                 )
202:                 .where(
203:                     InboundIntakeBox.tenant_id == tenant_id,
204:                     InboundIntakeRequest.tenant_id == tenant_id,
205:                     or_(
206:                         StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
207:                         and_(
208:                             InboundIntakeBox.storage_location_id.is_(None),
209:                             InboundIntakeRequest.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
210:                         ),
211:                     ),
212:                 )
213:             )
214:         ).all()
215:     )
216:     cargo_places = list(
217:         (
218:             await session.scalars(
219:                 select(InboundIntakeCargoPlace)
220:                 .join(InboundIntakeRequest)
221:                 .outerjoin(
222:                     StorageLocation,
223:                     StorageLocation.id
224:                     == InboundIntakeCargoPlace.storage_location_id,
225:                 )
226:                 .where(
227:                     InboundIntakeCargoPlace.tenant_id == tenant_id,
228:                     InboundIntakeRequest.tenant_id == tenant_id,
229:                     or_(
230:                         StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
231:                         and_(
232:                             InboundIntakeCargoPlace.storage_location_id.is_(None),
233:                             InboundIntakeRequest.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
234:                         ),
235:                     ),
236:                 )
237:             )
238:         ).all()
239:     )
240:     return warehouse_boxes, inbound_boxes, cargo_places
241: 
242: 
243: async def _load_map_rows(
244:     session: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, warehouse_id: uuid.UUID
245: ) -> tuple[
246:     list[tuple[InventoryBalance, StorageLocation, Product, Seller | None]],
247:     list[StorageLocation],
248:     list[Pallet],
249:     list[WarehouseBox],
250:     list[InboundIntakeBox],
251:     list[InboundIntakeCargoPlace],
252:     list[PendingInboundContent],
253:     dict[uuid.UUID, str | None],
254:     dict[tuple[uuid.UUID, int], SellerWildberriesImportedCard],
255: ]:
256:     rows = cast(
257:         list[tuple[InventoryBalance, StorageLocation, Product, Seller | None]],
258:         list(
259:             (
260:                 await session.execute(
261:                     select(InventoryBalance, StorageLocation, Product, Seller)
262:                     .join(
263:                         StorageLocation, StorageLocation.id == InventoryBalance.storage_location_id
264:                     )
265:                     .join(Product, Product.id == InventoryBalance.product_id)
266:                     .outerjoin(Seller, Seller.id == Product.seller_id)
267:                     .where(
268:                         InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
269:                         InventoryBalance.quantity > 0,
270:                         StorageLocation.tenant_id == tenant_id,
271:                         StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
272:                         Product.tenant_id == tenant_id,
273:                     )
274:                 )
275:             ).all()
276:         ),
277:     )
278:     locations = list(
279:         (
280:             await session.scalars(
281:                 select(StorageLocation)
282:                 .where(
283:                     StorageLocation.tenant_id == tenant_id,
284:                     StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
285:                     StorageLocation.deleted_at.is_(None),
286:                 )
287:                 .order_by(StorageLocation.code)
288:             )
289:         ).all()
290:     )
291:     pallets = list(
292:         (
293:             await session.scalars(
294:                 select(Pallet)
295:                 .where(
296:                     Pallet.tenant_id == tenant_id,
297:                     Pallet.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
298:                     Pallet.disbanded_at.is_(None),
299:                 )
300:                 .order_by(Pallet.code)
301:             )
302:         ).all()
303:     )
304:     warehouse_boxes, inbound_boxes, cargo_places = await _load_boxes(
305:         session, tenant_id, warehouse_id
306:     )
307:     pending_contents: list[PendingInboundContent] = []
308:     box_line_rows = await session.execute(
309:         select(InboundIntakeBoxLine, InboundIntakeBox, Product, Seller)
310:         .join(InboundIntakeBox, InboundIntakeBox.id == InboundIntakeBoxLine.box_id)
311:         .join(
312:             InboundIntakeRequest,
313:             InboundIntakeRequest.id == InboundIntakeBox.request_id,
314:         )
315:         .outerjoin(
316:             StorageLocation,
317:             StorageLocation.id == InboundIntakeBox.storage_location_id,
318:         )
319:         .join(Product, Product.id == InboundIntakeBoxLine.product_id)
320:         .outerjoin(Seller, Seller.id == Product.seller_id)
321:         .where(
322:             InboundIntakeBox.tenant_id == tenant_id,
323:             InboundIntakeRequest.tenant_id == tenant_id,
324:             or_(
325:                 StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
326:                 and_(
327:                     InboundIntakeBox.storage_location_id.is_(None),
328:                     InboundIntakeRequest.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
329:                 ),
330:             ),
331:             InboundIntakeBoxLine.quantity > InboundIntakeBoxLine.posted_qty,
332:             ~exists(
333:                 select(InventoryBalance.id).where(
334:                     InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
335:                     InventoryBalance.container_kind == "box",
336:                     InventoryBalance.container_id == InboundIntakeBox.id,
337:                     InventoryBalance.product_id == InboundIntakeBoxLine.product_id,
338:                 )
339:             ),
340:         )
341:     )
342:     pending_contents.extend(
343:         PendingInboundContent(
344:             kind="box",
345:             container_id=box.id,
346:             line_id=line.id,
347:             product=product,
348:             seller=seller,
349:             quantity=max(0, int(line.quantity) - int(line.posted_qty)),
350:         )
351:         for line, box, product, seller in box_line_rows.all()
352:     )
353:     cargo_line_rows = await session.execute(
354:         select(
355:             InboundIntakeCargoPlaceLine,
356:             InboundIntakeCargoPlace,
357:             Product,
358:             Seller,
359:         )
360:         .join(
361:             InboundIntakeCargoPlace,
362:             InboundIntakeCargoPlace.id
363:             == InboundIntakeCargoPlaceLine.cargo_place_id,
364:         )
365:         .join(
366:             InboundIntakeRequest,
367:             InboundIntakeRequest.id == InboundIntakeCargoPlace.request_id,
368:         )
369:         .outerjoin(
370:             StorageLocation,
371:             StorageLocation.id == InboundIntakeCargoPlace.storage_location_id,
372:         )
373:         .join(Product, Product.id == InboundIntakeCargoPlaceLine.product_id)
374:         .outerjoin(Seller, Seller.id == Product.seller_id)
375:         .where(
376:             InboundIntakeCargoPlace.tenant_id == tenant_id,
377:             InboundIntakeRequest.tenant_id == tenant_id,
378:             or_(
379:                 StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
380:                 and_(
381:                     InboundIntakeCargoPlace.storage_location_id.is_(None),
382:                     InboundIntakeRequest.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
383:                 ),
384:             ),
385:             InboundIntakeCargoPlaceLine.quantity
386:             > InboundIntakeCargoPlaceLine.posted_qty,
387:             ~exists(
388:                 select(InventoryBalance.id).where(
389:                     InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
390:                     InventoryBalance.container_kind == "cargo_place",
391:                     InventoryBalance.container_id == InboundIntakeCargoPlace.id,
392:                     InventoryBalance.product_id
393:                     == InboundIntakeCargoPlaceLine.product_id,
394:                 )
395:             ),
396:         )
397:     )
398:     pending_contents.extend(
399:         PendingInboundContent(
400:             kind="cargo_place",
401:             container_id=place.id,
402:             line_id=line.id,
403:             product=product,
404:             seller=seller,
405:             quantity=max(0, int(line.quantity) - int(line.posted_qty)),
406:         )
407:         for line, place, product, seller in cargo_line_rows.all()
408:     )
409:     request_ids = {
410:         *(box.request_id for box in inbound_boxes),
411:         *(place.request_id for place in cargo_places),
412:         *(
413:             pallet.inbound_request_id
414:             for pallet in pallets
415:             if pallet.inbound_request_id is not None
416:         ),
417:         *(
418:             box.inbound_request_id
419:             for box in warehouse_boxes
420:             if box.inbound_request_id is not None
421:         ),
422:     }
423:     request_numbers: dict[uuid.UUID, str | None] = {}
424:     if request_ids:
425:         request_rows = await session.execute(
426:             select(
427:                 InboundIntakeRequest.id,
428:                 InboundIntakeRequest.display_number,
429:                 InboundIntakeRequest.document_number,
430:             ).where(
431:                 InboundIntakeRequest.id.in_(request_ids),
432:                 InboundIntakeRequest.tenant_id == tenant_id,
433:             )
434:         )
435:         request_numbers = {
436:             request_id: display_number or document_number
437:             for request_id, display_number, document_number in request_rows.all()
438:         }
439:     pairs = {
440:         (product.seller_id, product.wb_nm_id)
441:         for _balance, _location, product, _seller in rows
442:         if product.seller_id is not None and product.wb_nm_id is not None
443:     }
444:     pairs.update(
445:         (row.product.seller_id, row.product.wb_nm_id)
446:         for row in pending_contents
447:         if row.product.seller_id is not None and row.product.wb_nm_id is not None
448:     )
449:     cards: dict[tuple[uuid.UUID, int], SellerWildberriesImportedCard] = {}
450:     if pairs:
451:         seller_ids = {pair[0] for pair in pairs}
452:         nm_ids = {pair[1] for pair in pairs}
453:         card_rows = await session.scalars(
454:             select(SellerWildberriesImportedCard).where(
455:                 SellerWildberriesImportedCard.tenant_id == tenant_id,
456:                 SellerWildberriesImportedCard.seller_id.in_(seller_ids),
457:                 SellerWildberriesImportedCard.nm_id.in_(nm_ids),
458:             )
459:         )
460:         cards = {(card.seller_id, int(card.nm_id)): card for card in card_rows.all()}
461:     return (
462:         rows,
463:         locations,
464:         pallets,
465:         warehouse_boxes,
466:         inbound_boxes,
467:         cargo_places,
468:         pending_contents,
469:         request_numbers,
470:         cards,
471:     )
472: 
473: 
474: def _normalize_container(node: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
475:     children = [
476:         _normalize_container(child) if child["kind"] != "product" else child
477:         for child in node["children"]
478:     ]
479:     sellers: set[str] = set()
480: 
481:     def collect(child: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
482:         if child["kind"] == "product":
483:             if child["seller_name"]:
484:                 sellers.add(child["seller_name"])
485:             return
486:         for nested in child["children"]:
487:             collect(nested)
488: 
489:     for child in children:
490:         collect(child)
491:     return {
492:         **node,
493:         "children": children,
494:         "qty": sum(int(child["qty"]) for child in children),
495:         "seller_name": next(iter(sellers)) if len(sellers) == 1 else None,
496:     }
497: 
498: 
499: async def get_warehouse_map(
500:     session: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID, warehouse_id: uuid.UUID
501: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
502:     await _assert_warehouse(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id)
503:     address_enabled = await is_address_storage_enabled(session, tenant_id)
504:     (
505:         rows,
506:         locations,
507:         pallets,
508:         warehouse_boxes,
509:         inbound_boxes,
510:         cargo_places,
511:         pending_contents,
512:         request_numbers,
513:         cards,
514:     ) = await _load_map_rows(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id)
515:     # У озоновского товара снапшота карточки WB нет — фото лежит в привязке Ozon.
516:     ozon_photos = await load_ozon_primary_image_urls(
517:         session,
518:         tenant_id,
519:         {product.id for _balance, _location, product, _seller in rows}
520:         | {row.product.id for row in pending_contents},
521:     )
522: 
523:     location_by_id = {location.id: location for location in locations}
524:     balance_location: dict[tuple[str, uuid.UUID], uuid.UUID] = {}
525:     for balance, location, _product, _seller in rows:
526:         if balance.container_kind and balance.container_id:
527:             balance_location.setdefault((balance.container_kind, balance.container_id), location.id)
528: 
529:     nodes: dict[tuple[str, uuid.UUID], dict[str, Any]] = {}
530:     holders: dict[tuple[str, uuid.UUID], tuple[str, uuid.UUID] | uuid.UUID | None] = {}
531: 
532:     def source_document_number(request_id: uuid.UUID | None) -> str | None:
533:         return request_numbers.get(request_id) if request_id is not None else None
534: 
535:     pallet_ids = {pallet.id for pallet in pallets}
536:     for pallet in pallets:
537:         key = ("pallet", pallet.id)
538:         nodes[key] = {
539:             "kind": "pallet",
540:             "id": str(pallet.id),
541:             "code": pallet.code,
542:             "barcode": pallet.barcode,
543:             "seller_name": None,
544:             "qty": 0,
545:             "source_document_number": source_document_number(
546:                 pallet.inbound_request_id
547:             ),
548:             "children": [],
549:         }
550:         holders[key] = pallet.storage_location_id
551:     for box in warehouse_boxes:
552:         key = (box.container_kind, box.id)
553:         nodes[key] = {
554:             "kind": box.container_kind,
555:             "id": str(box.id),
556:             "code": box.internal_barcode,
557:             "barcode": box.internal_barcode,
558:             "seller_name": None,
559:             "qty": 0,
560:             "source_document_number": source_document_number(box.inbound_request_id),
561:             "children": [],
562:         }
563:         holders[key] = (
564:             ("pallet", box.pallet_id)
565:             if box.pallet_id in pallet_ids
566:             else box.storage_location_id or balance_location.get(key)
567:         )
568:     for inbound_box in inbound_boxes:
569:         key = ("box", inbound_box.id)
570:         nodes[key] = {
571:             "kind": "box",
572:             "id": str(inbound_box.id),
573:             "code": f"КР-{inbound_box.box_number:06d}",
574:             "barcode": inbound_box.internal_barcode,
575:             "seller_name": None,
576:             "qty": 0,
577:             "source_document_number": request_numbers.get(inbound_box.request_id),
578:             "children": [],
579:         }
580:         holders[key] = (
581:             ("pallet", inbound_box.pallet_id)
582:             if inbound_box.pallet_id in pallet_ids
583:             else inbound_box.storage_location_id or balance_location.get(key)
584:         )
585:     for place in cargo_places:
586:         key = ("cargo_place", place.id)
587:         nodes[key] = {
588:             "kind": "cargo_place",
589:             "id": str(place.id),
590:             "code": f"ГМ-{place.place_number:06d}",
591:             "barcode": place.internal_barcode,
592:             "seller_name": None,
593:             "qty": 0,
594:             "source_document_number": request_numbers.get(place.request_id),
595:             "children": [],
596:         }
597:         holders[key] = (
598:             ("pallet", place.pallet_id)
599:             if place.pallet_id in pallet_ids
600:             else place.storage_location_id or balance_location.get(key)
601:         )
602: 
603:     loose_by_location: dict[uuid.UUID, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
604:     current_container_qty: dict[tuple[str, uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID], int] = defaultdict(int)
605:     sellers: set[str] = set()
606:     categories: set[str] = set()
607:     for balance, location, product, seller in rows:
608:         card = (
609:             cards.get((product.seller_id, product.wb_nm_id))
610:             if (product.seller_id is not None and product.wb_nm_id is not None)
611:             else None
612:         )
613:         category, photo_url = _card_data(card)
614:         photo_url = photo_url or ozon_photos.get(product.id)
615:         seller_name = seller.name if seller is not None else None
616:         if seller_name:
617:             sellers.add(seller_name)
618:         if category:
619:             categories.add(category)
620:         product_node = {
621:             "kind": "product",
622:             "id": str(balance.id),
623:             "product_id": str(product.id),
624:             "name": product.name,
625:             "seller_name": seller_name,
626:             "category": category,
627:             "barcode": product.wb_barcode,
628:             "seller_article": product.wb_vendor_code,
629:             "photo_url": photo_url,
630:             "qty": int(balance.quantity),
631:         }
632:         container_key = (
633:             (balance.container_kind, balance.container_id)
634:             if balance.container_kind and balance.container_id
635:             else None
636:         )
637:         if container_key in nodes:
638:             assert container_key is not None
639:             nodes[container_key]["children"].append(product_node)
640:             current_container_qty[
641:                 (container_key[0], container_key[1], product.id)
642:             ] += int(balance.quantity)
643:         else:
644:             loose_by_location[location.id].append(product_node)
645: 
646:     for pending in pending_contents:
647:         container_key = (pending.kind, pending.container_id)
648:         if container_key not in nodes:
649:             continue
650:         pending_quantity = max(
651:             0,
652:             pending.quantity
653:             - current_container_qty.get(
654:                 (pending.kind, pending.container_id, pending.product.id), 0
655:             ),
656:         )
657:         if pending_quantity == 0:
658:             continue
659:         product = pending.product
660:         card = (
661:             cards.get((product.seller_id, product.wb_nm_id))
662:             if (product.seller_id is not None and product.wb_nm_id is not None)
663:             else None
664:         )
665:         category, photo_url = _card_data(card)
666:         photo_url = photo_url or ozon_photos.get(product.id)
667:         seller_name = pending.seller.name if pending.seller is not None else None
668:         if seller_name:
669:             sellers.add(seller_name)
670:         if category:
671:             categories.add(category)
672:         nodes[container_key]["children"].append(
673:             {
674:                 "kind": "product",
675:                 "id": str(pending.line_id),
676:                 "product_id": str(product.id),
677:                 "name": product.name,
678:                 "seller_name": seller_name,
679:                 "category": category,
680:                 "barcode": product.wb_barcode,
681:                 "seller_article": product.wb_vendor_code,
682:                 "photo_url": photo_url,
683:                 "qty": pending_quantity,
684:             }
685:         )
686: 
687:     root_by_location: dict[uuid.UUID, list[dict[str, Any]]] = defaultdict(list)
688:     unassigned: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
689:     for key, node in nodes.items():
690:         holder = holders.get(key)
691:         if isinstance(holder, tuple) and holder in nodes:
692:             nodes[holder]["children"].append(node)
693:             continue
694:         root_location = location_by_id.get(holder) if isinstance(holder, uuid.UUID) else None
695:         if (
696:             address_enabled
697:             and root_location is not None
698:             and root_location.code != SORTING_LOCATION_CODE
699:         ):
700:             root_by_location[root_location.id].append(node)
701:         else:
702:             unassigned.append(node)
703: 
704:     cells: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
705:     if address_enabled:
706:         for location in locations:
707:             if location.code == SORTING_LOCATION_CODE:
708:                 unassigned.extend(loose_by_location.pop(location.id, []))
709:                 continue
710:             children = [
711:                 *root_by_location.get(location.id, []),
712:                 *loose_by_location.pop(location.id, []),
713:             ]
714:             normalized = [
715:                 _normalize_container(child) if child["kind"] != "product" else child
716:                 for child in children
717:             ]
718:             cells.append(
719:                 {
720:                     "id": str(location.id),
721:                     "code": location.code,
722:                     "barcode": location.barcode,
723:                     "qty": sum(int(child["qty"]) for child in normalized),
724:                     "children": normalized,
725:                 }
726:             )
727:     else:
728:         for loose in loose_by_location.values():
729:             unassigned.extend(loose)
730:     normalized_unassigned = [
731:         _normalize_container(node) if node["kind"] != "product" else node for node in unassigned
732:     ]
733: 
734:     warehouses = list(
735:         (
736:             await session.scalars(
737:                 select(Warehouse)
738:                 .where(
739:                     Warehouse.tenant_id == tenant_id,
740:                     Warehouse.is_operational.is_(True),
741:                 )
742:                 .order_by(Warehouse.name)
743:             )
744:         ).all()
745:     )
746:     event_rows = list(
747:         (
748:             await session.execute(
749:                 select(WarehouseMapEvent, User)
750:                 .outerjoin(User, User.id == WarehouseMapEvent.actor_user_id)
751:                 .where(
752:                     WarehouseMapEvent.tenant_id == tenant_id,
753:                     WarehouseMapEvent.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
754:                 )
755:                 .order_by(WarehouseMapEvent.created_at.desc(), WarehouseMapEvent.id.desc())
756:                 .limit(100)
757:             )
758:         ).all()
759:     )
760:     journal = [
761:         {
762:             "id": str(event.id),
763:             "at": event.created_at.astimezone(UTC).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z"),
764:             "actor_name": actor.email if actor is not None else "Система",
765:             "subject": event.subject,
766:             "qty": event.quantity,
767:             "from_label": event.from_label,
768:             "to_label": event.to_label,
769:         }
770:         for event, actor in event_rows
771:     ]
772:     return {
773:         "warehouses": [{"id": str(row.id), "name": row.name} for row in warehouses],
774:         "sellers": sorted(sellers),
775:         "categories": sorted(categories),
776:         "cells": cells,
777:         "unassigned": normalized_unassigned,
778:         "journal": journal,
779:     }
780: 
781: 
782: async def _container_location_id(
783:     session: AsyncSession,
784:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
785:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
786:     kind: ContainerKind,
787:     container_id: uuid.UUID,
788: ) -> uuid.UUID:
789:     await validate_container(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, kind, container_id)
790:     if kind == "pallet":
791:         pallet = await session.get(Pallet, container_id)
792:         assert pallet is not None
793:         if pallet.storage_location_id is not None:
794:             return pallet.storage_location_id
795:     if kind == "box":
796:         box = await session.get(WarehouseBox, container_id)
797:         if (
798:             box is not None
799:             and box.tenant_id == tenant_id
800:             and box.warehouse_id == warehouse_id
801:             and box.container_kind == kind
802:         ):
803:             if box.pallet_id is not None:
804:                 pallet = await session.get(Pallet, box.pallet_id)
805:                 if pallet is not None and pallet.storage_location_id is not None:
806:                     return pallet.storage_location_id
807:             if box.storage_location_id is not None:
808:                 return box.storage_location_id
809:         inbound_box = await session.get(InboundIntakeBox, container_id)
810:         if inbound_box is not None and inbound_box.tenant_id == tenant_id:
811:             if inbound_box.pallet_id is not None:
812:                 pallet = await session.get(Pallet, inbound_box.pallet_id)
813:                 if pallet is not None and pallet.storage_location_id is not None:
814:                     return pallet.storage_location_id
815:             if inbound_box.storage_location_id is not None:
816:                 return inbound_box.storage_location_id
817:     if kind == "cargo_place":
818:         cargo_place = await session.get(WarehouseBox, container_id)
819:         if (
820:             cargo_place is not None
821:             and cargo_place.tenant_id == tenant_id
822:             and cargo_place.warehouse_id == warehouse_id
823:             and cargo_place.container_kind == kind
824:         ):
825:             if cargo_place.pallet_id is not None:
826:                 pallet = await session.get(Pallet, cargo_place.pallet_id)
827:                 if pallet is not None and pallet.storage_location_id is not None:
828:                     return pallet.storage_location_id
829:             if cargo_place.storage_location_id is not None:
830:                 return cargo_place.storage_location_id
831:         inbound_cargo = await session.get(InboundIntakeCargoPlace, container_id)
832:         if inbound_cargo is not None and inbound_cargo.tenant_id == tenant_id:
833:             if inbound_cargo.pallet_id is not None:
834:                 pallet = await session.get(Pallet, inbound_cargo.pallet_id)
835:                 if pallet is not None and pallet.storage_location_id is not None:
836:                     return pallet.storage_location_id
837:             if inbound_cargo.storage_location_id is not None:
838:                 return inbound_cargo.storage_location_id
839:     balance_location = await session.scalar(
840:         select(InventoryBalance.storage_location_id)
841:         .where(
842:             InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
843:             InventoryBalance.container_kind == kind,
844:             InventoryBalance.container_id == container_id,
845:             InventoryBalance.quantity > 0,
846:         )
847:         .limit(1)
848:     )
849:     if balance_location is not None:
850:         location = await session.get(StorageLocation, balance_location)
851:         if location is not None and location.warehouse_id == warehouse_id:
852:             return balance_location
853:     sorting = await get_or_create_sorting_location(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id)
854:     return sorting.id
855: 
856: 
857: async def resolve_container_location(
858:     session: AsyncSession,
859:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
860:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
861:     kind: ContainerKind,
862:     container_id: uuid.UUID,
863: ) -> uuid.UUID:
864:     """Return the persisted root location for a pick-source container."""
865:     return await _container_location_id(
866:         session,
867:         tenant_id,
868:         warehouse_id,
869:         kind,
870:         container_id,
871:     )

873: 
874: async def _destination(
875:     session: AsyncSession,
876:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
877:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
878:     to_kind: DestinationKind,
879:     to_id: uuid.UUID | None,
880: ) -> tuple[uuid.UUID, ContainerKind | None, uuid.UUID | None, str]:
881:     if to_kind == "cell":
882:         if not await is_address_storage_enabled(session, tenant_id):
883:             raise WarehouseMapError("address_storage_disabled")
884:         if to_id is None:
885:             raise WarehouseMapError("destination_required")
886:         location = await session.get(StorageLocation, to_id)
887:         if (
888:             location is None
889:             or location.tenant_id != tenant_id
890:             or location.warehouse_id != warehouse_id
891:             or location.deleted_at is not None
892:             or location.code == SORTING_LOCATION_CODE
893:         ):
894:             raise WarehouseMapError("cell_not_found")
895:         return location.id, None, None, f"Ячейка {location.code}"
896:     if to_kind in {"unassigned", "sorting"}:
897:         sorting = await get_or_create_sorting_location(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id)
898:         return sorting.id, None, None, UNASSIGNED_LABEL
899:     if to_id is None:
900:         raise WarehouseMapError("destination_required")
901:     container_kind = cast(ContainerKind, to_kind)
902:     try:
903:         location_id = await _container_location_id(
904:             session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, container_kind, to_id
905:         )
906:     except ValueError as exc:
907:         raise WarehouseMapError("destination_not_found") from exc
908:     code = await _container_code(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, container_kind, to_id)
909:     return location_id, container_kind, to_id, _container_title(container_kind, code)
910: 
911: 
912: async def _container_code(
913:     session: AsyncSession,
914:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
915:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
916:     kind: ContainerKind,
917:     container_id: uuid.UUID,
918: ) -> str:
919:     if kind == "pallet":
920:         row = await session.get(Pallet, container_id)
921:         if row is not None and row.tenant_id == tenant_id and row.warehouse_id == warehouse_id:
922:             return row.code
923:     elif kind == "box":
924:         warehouse_box = await session.get(WarehouseBox, container_id)
925:         if (
926:             warehouse_box is not None
927:             and warehouse_box.tenant_id == tenant_id
928:             and warehouse_box.warehouse_id == warehouse_id
929:             and warehouse_box.container_kind == "box"
930:         ):
931:             return warehouse_box.internal_barcode
932:         inbound = await session.get(InboundIntakeBox, container_id)
933:         if inbound is not None and inbound.tenant_id == tenant_id:
934:             try:
935:                 await validate_container(
936:                     session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, "box", container_id
937:                 )
938:             except ValueError:
939:                 pass
940:             else:
941:                 return f"КР-{inbound.box_number:06d}"
942:     else:
943:         warehouse_cargo_place = await session.get(WarehouseBox, container_id)
944:         if (
945:             warehouse_cargo_place is not None
946:             and warehouse_cargo_place.tenant_id == tenant_id
947:             and warehouse_cargo_place.warehouse_id == warehouse_id
948:             and warehouse_cargo_place.container_kind == "cargo_place"
949:         ):
950:             return warehouse_cargo_place.internal_barcode
951:         cargo = await session.get(InboundIntakeCargoPlace, container_id)
952:         if cargo is not None and cargo.tenant_id == tenant_id:
953:             try:
954:                 await validate_container(
955:                     session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, "cargo_place", container_id
956:                 )
957:             except ValueError:
958:                 pass
959:             else:
960:                 return f"ГМ-{cargo.place_number:06d}"
961:     raise WarehouseMapError("object_not_found")
962: 
963: 
964: async def _location_label(session: AsyncSession, location_id: uuid.UUID) -> str:
965:     location = await session.get(StorageLocation, location_id)
966:     if location is None or location.code == SORTING_LOCATION_CODE:
967:         return UNASSIGNED_LABEL
968:     return f"Ячейка {location.code}"

971: async def _transfer_balance(
972:     session: AsyncSession,
973:     *,
974:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
975:     balance: InventoryBalance,
976:     quantity: int,
977:     destination_location_id: uuid.UUID,
978:     destination_container_kind: ContainerKind | None,
979:     destination_container_id: uuid.UUID | None,
980:     transfer_group_id: uuid.UUID,
981:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID,
982: ) -> None:
983:     source_kind = cast(ContainerKind | None, balance.container_kind)
984:     await inventory_service.record_movement_and_adjust_balance(
985:         session,
986:         tenant_id=tenant_id,
987:         product_id=balance.product_id,
988:         storage_location_id=balance.storage_location_id,
989:         quantity_delta=-quantity,
990:         movement_type=MOVEMENT_TYPE_WAREHOUSE_MAP,
991:         transfer_group_id=transfer_group_id,
992:         container_kind=source_kind,
993:         container_id=balance.container_id,
994:         actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
995:     )
996:     await inventory_service.record_movement_and_adjust_balance(
997:         session,
998:         tenant_id=tenant_id,
999:         product_id=balance.product_id,
1000:         storage_location_id=destination_location_id,
1001:         quantity_delta=quantity,
1002:         movement_type=MOVEMENT_TYPE_WAREHOUSE_MAP,
1003:         transfer_group_id=transfer_group_id,
1004:         container_kind=destination_container_kind,
1005:         container_id=destination_container_id,
1006:         actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
1007:     )

1009: 
1010: async def _container_balances(
1011:     session: AsyncSession,
1012:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1013:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
1014:     kind: ContainerKind,
1015:     container_id: uuid.UUID,
1016: ) -> list[InventoryBalance]:
1017:     refs: list[tuple[str, uuid.UUID]] = [(kind, container_id)]
1018:     if kind == "pallet":
1019:         warehouse_boxes, inbound_boxes, cargo_places = await _load_boxes(
1020:             session, tenant_id, warehouse_id
1021:         )
1022:         refs.extend(
1023:             (row.container_kind, row.id)
1024:             for row in warehouse_boxes
1025:             if row.pallet_id == container_id
1026:         )
1027:         refs.extend(("box", row.id) for row in inbound_boxes if row.pallet_id == container_id)
1028:         refs.extend(
1029:             ("cargo_place", row.id) for row in cargo_places if row.pallet_id == container_id
1030:         )
1031:     predicates = [
1032:         (InventoryBalance.container_kind == ref_kind) & (InventoryBalance.container_id == ref_id)
1033:         for ref_kind, ref_id in refs
1034:     ]
1035:     rows = list(
1036:         (
1037:             await session.scalars(
1038:                 select(InventoryBalance)
1039:                 .join(StorageLocation)
1040:                 .where(
1041:                     InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
1042:                     InventoryBalance.quantity > 0,
1043:                     StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
1044:                     or_(*predicates),
1045:                 )
1046:                 .with_for_update()
1047:             )
1048:         ).all()
1049:     )
1050:     return rows
1051: 
1052: 
1053: async def _place_container(
1054:     session: AsyncSession,
1055:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1056:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
1057:     kind: ContainerKind,
1058:     container_id: uuid.UUID,
1059:     to_kind: DestinationKind,
1060:     to_id: uuid.UUID | None,
1061:     destination_location_id: uuid.UUID,
1062: ) -> None:
1063:     if kind == "pallet":
1064:         if to_kind not in {"cell", "unassigned", "sorting"}:
1065:             raise WarehouseMapError("invalid_container_destination")
1066:         pallet = await session.get(Pallet, container_id)
1067:         if (
1068:             pallet is None
1069:             or pallet.tenant_id != tenant_id
1070:             or pallet.warehouse_id != warehouse_id
1071:             or pallet.disbanded_at is not None
1072:         ):
1073:             raise WarehouseMapError("object_not_found")
1074:         pallet.storage_location_id = destination_location_id if to_kind == "cell" else None
1075:         return
1076:     if to_kind not in {"cell", "unassigned", "sorting", "pallet"}:
1077:         raise WarehouseMapError("invalid_container_destination")
1078:     pallet_id = to_id if to_kind == "pallet" else None
1079:     if pallet_id == container_id:
1080:         raise WarehouseMapError("container_cycle")
1081:     if kind == "box":
1082:         warehouse_box = await session.get(WarehouseBox, container_id)
1083:         if (
1084:             warehouse_box is not None
1085:             and warehouse_box.tenant_id == tenant_id
1086:             and warehouse_box.warehouse_id == warehouse_id
1087:             and warehouse_box.container_kind == "box"
1088:         ):
1089:             warehouse_box.pallet_id = pallet_id
1090:             warehouse_box.storage_location_id = (
1091:                 destination_location_id if to_kind == "cell" else None
1092:             )
1093:             return
1094:         inbound = await session.get(InboundIntakeBox, container_id)
1095:         if inbound is None or inbound.tenant_id != tenant_id:
1096:             raise WarehouseMapError("object_not_found")
1097:         try:
1098:             await validate_container(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, "box", container_id)
1099:         except ValueError as exc:
1100:             raise WarehouseMapError("object_not_found") from exc
1101:         inbound.pallet_id = pallet_id
1102:         inbound.storage_location_id = destination_location_id
1103:         return
1104:     warehouse_cargo_place = await session.get(WarehouseBox, container_id)
1105:     if (
1106:         warehouse_cargo_place is not None
1107:         and warehouse_cargo_place.tenant_id == tenant_id
1108:         and warehouse_cargo_place.warehouse_id == warehouse_id
1109:         and warehouse_cargo_place.container_kind == "cargo_place"
1110:     ):
1111:         warehouse_cargo_place.pallet_id = pallet_id
1112:         warehouse_cargo_place.storage_location_id = (
1113:             destination_location_id if to_kind == "cell" else None
1114:         )
1115:         return
1116:     cargo = await session.get(InboundIntakeCargoPlace, container_id)
1117:     if cargo is None or cargo.tenant_id != tenant_id:
1118:         raise WarehouseMapError("object_not_found")
1119:     try:
1120:         await validate_container(
1121:             session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, "cargo_place", container_id
1122:         )
1123:     except ValueError as exc:
1124:         raise WarehouseMapError("object_not_found") from exc
1125:     cargo.pallet_id = pallet_id
1126:     cargo.storage_location_id = destination_location_id
1127: 
1128: 
1129: async def move_object(
1130:     session: AsyncSession,
1131:     *,
1132:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1133:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
1134:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID,
1135:     kind: ObjectKind,
1136:     object_id: uuid.UUID,
1137:     to_kind: DestinationKind,
1138:     to_id: uuid.UUID | None,
1139:     quantity: int | None,
1140:     commit: bool = True,
1141: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
1142:     await _assert_warehouse(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id)
1143:     # Количество имеет смысл только для товара: тара всегда переезжает целиком
1144:     # вместе с содержимым (контракт карты склада, раздел 3.1).
1145:     if kind == "product" and (quantity is None or quantity <= 0):
1146:         raise WarehouseMapError("quantity_must_be_positive")
1147:     if kind == "pallet" and to_kind == "pallet" and object_id == to_id:
1148:         raise WarehouseMapError("container_cycle")
1149:     destination_location_id, destination_kind, destination_id, to_label = await _destination(
1150:         session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, to_kind, to_id
1151:     )
1152:     transfer_group_id = uuid.uuid4()
1153: 
1154:     if kind == "product":
1155:         balance = await session.scalar(
1156:             select(InventoryBalance)
1157:             .join(StorageLocation)
1158:             .where(
1159:                 InventoryBalance.id == object_id,
1160:                 InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
1161:                 InventoryBalance.quantity > 0,
1162:                 StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
1163:             )
1164:             .with_for_update()
1165:         )
1166:         if balance is None:
1167:             raise WarehouseMapError("object_not_found")
1168:         # Проверка выше уже гарантировала число для товара; сообщаем это типам.
1169:         assert quantity is not None
1170:         if quantity > balance.quantity:
1171:             raise WarehouseMapError("insufficient_stock")
1172:         product = await session.get(Product, balance.product_id)
1173:         assert product is not None
1174:         from_label = await _location_label(session, balance.storage_location_id)
1175:         if balance.container_kind and balance.container_id:
1176:             code = await _container_code(
1177:                 session,
1178:                 tenant_id,
1179:                 warehouse_id,
1180:                 cast(ContainerKind, balance.container_kind),
1181:                 balance.container_id,
1182:             )
1183:             from_label = _container_title(balance.container_kind, code)
1184:         await _transfer_balance(
1185:             session,
1186:             tenant_id=tenant_id,
1187:             balance=balance,
1188:             quantity=quantity,
1189:             destination_location_id=destination_location_id,
1190:             destination_container_kind=destination_kind,
1191:             destination_container_id=destination_id,
1192:             transfer_group_id=transfer_group_id,
1193:             actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
1194:         )
1195:         subject = product.name
1196:         moved_quantity: int | None = quantity
1197:     else:
1198:         container_kind: ContainerKind = kind
1199:         try:
1200:             await validate_container(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, container_kind, object_id)
1201:         except ValueError as exc:
1202:             raise WarehouseMapError("object_not_found") from exc
1203:         if to_kind in {"box", "cargo_place"}:
1204:             raise WarehouseMapError("invalid_container_destination")
1205:         source_location_id = await _container_location_id(
1206:             session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, container_kind, object_id
1207:         )
1208:         from_label = await _location_label(session, source_location_id)
1209:         balances = await _container_balances(
1210:             session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, container_kind, object_id
1211:         )
1212:         if (
1213:             balances
1214:             and source_location_id == destination_location_id
1215:             and to_kind in {"cell", "sorting", "unassigned"}
1216:         ):
1217:             raise WarehouseMapError("nothing_to_move")
1218:         moved_total = sum(int(row.quantity) for row in balances)
1219:         pending_moved: int | None = None
1220:         try:
1221:             pending_moved = (
1222:                 await inbound_container_putaway_service.putaway_pending_container(
1223:                     session,
1224:                     tenant_id=tenant_id,
1225:                     warehouse_id=warehouse_id,
1226:                     actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
1227:                     kind=container_kind,
1228:                     container_id=object_id,
1229:                     destination_location_id=destination_location_id,
1230:                     destination_is_cell=to_kind == "cell",
1231:                 )
1232:             )
1233:         except inbound_container_putaway_service.InboundContainerPutawayError as exc:
1234:             # A container with current balance can be moved again after its
1235:             # original intake has already been posted. The canonical intake
1236:             # bridge is required only while that intake still has pending qty.
1237:             if not balances or exc.code != "nothing_to_move":
1238:                 raise WarehouseMapError(exc.code) from exc
1239:         if pending_moved is not None:
1240:             moved_total = pending_moved
1241:         else:
1242:             for balance in balances:
1243:                 await _transfer_balance(
1244:                     session,
1245:                     tenant_id=tenant_id,
1246:                     balance=balance,
1247:                     quantity=int(balance.quantity),
1248:                     destination_location_id=destination_location_id,
1249:                     destination_container_kind=cast(ContainerKind, balance.container_kind),
1250:                     destination_container_id=balance.container_id,
1251:                     transfer_group_id=transfer_group_id,
1252:                     actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
1253:                 )
1254:         await _place_container(
1255:             session,
1256:             tenant_id,
1257:             warehouse_id,
1258:             container_kind,
1259:             object_id,
1260:             to_kind,
1261:             to_id,
1262:             destination_location_id,
1263:         )
1264:         code = await _container_code(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id, container_kind, object_id)
1265:         subject = _container_title(container_kind, code)
1266:         moved_quantity = moved_total or None
1267: 
1268:     event = WarehouseMapEvent(
1269:         tenant_id=tenant_id,
1270:         warehouse_id=warehouse_id,
1271:         actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
1272:         subject=subject,
1273:         quantity=moved_quantity,
1274:         from_label=from_label,
1275:         to_label=to_label,
1276:     )
1277:     session.add(event)
1278:     if commit:
1279:         await session.commit()
1280:     else:
1281:         await session.flush()
1282:     return {"id": str(event.id), "moved_qty": moved_quantity}
1283: 
1284: 
1285: async def create_sorting_object(
1286:     session: AsyncSession,
1287:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1288:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
1289:     *,
1290:     kind: Literal["pallet", "box", "cargo_place"],
1291:     inbound_request_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
1292:     storage_location_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
1293:     commit: bool = True,
1294: ) -> dict[str, str | None]:
1295:     await _assert_warehouse(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id)
1296:     if inbound_request_id is not None:
1297:         request = await session.get(InboundIntakeRequest, inbound_request_id)
1298:         if (
1299:             request is None
1300:             or request.tenant_id != tenant_id
1301:             or request.warehouse_id != warehouse_id
1302:         ):
1303:             raise WarehouseMapError("inbound_request_not_found")
1304:     if storage_location_id is not None:
1305:         # WMS-153: тара должна попадать сразу в выбранную ячейку, а не в общий
1306:         # склад. Проверяем, что ячейка принадлежит тому же складу и тенанту —
1307:         # иначе оператор случайно создаст тару чужого склада.
1308:         location = await session.get(StorageLocation, storage_location_id)
1309:         if (
1310:             location is None
1311:             or location.tenant_id != tenant_id
1312:             or location.warehouse_id != warehouse_id
1313:             or location.deleted_at is not None
1314:         ):
1315:             raise WarehouseMapError("storage_location_not_found")
1316:     if kind == "pallet":
1317:         try:
1318:             pallet = await pallet_service.create_pallet(
1319:                 session,
1320:                 tenant_id,
1321:                 warehouse_id=warehouse_id,
1322:                 storage_location_id=storage_location_id,
1323:                 inbound_request_id=inbound_request_id,
1324:                 commit=commit,
1325:             )
1326:         except pallet_service.PalletServiceError as exc:
1327:             raise WarehouseMapError(exc.code) from exc
1328:         return {
1329:             "id": str(pallet.id),
1330:             "kind": kind,
1331:             "code": pallet.code,
1332:             "barcode": pallet.barcode,
1333:             "holder": None,
1334:         }
1335: 
1336:     try:
1337:         container = await warehouse_box_service.create_warehouse_box(
1338:             session,
1339:             tenant_id,
1340:             warehouse_id=warehouse_id,
1341:             storage_location_id=storage_location_id,
1342:             inbound_request_id=inbound_request_id,
1343:             container_kind=kind,
1344:         )
1345:         if commit:
1346:             await session.commit()
1347:             await session.refresh(container)
1348:     except warehouse_box_service.WarehouseBoxError as exc:
1349:         await session.rollback()
1350:         raise WarehouseMapError(exc.code) from exc
1351:     return {
1352:         "id": str(container.id),
1353:         "kind": kind,
1354:         "code": container.internal_barcode,
1355:         "barcode": container.internal_barcode,
1356:         "holder": None,
1357:     }
1358: 
1359: 
1360: async def disband_pallet(
1361:     session: AsyncSession,
1362:     *,
1363:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1364:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
1365:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID,
1366:     pallet_id: uuid.UUID,
1367: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
1368:     await _assert_warehouse(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id)
1369:     pallet = await session.get(Pallet, pallet_id)
1370:     if (
1371:         pallet is None
1372:         or pallet.tenant_id != tenant_id
1373:         or pallet.warehouse_id != warehouse_id
1374:         or pallet.disbanded_at is not None
1375:     ):
1376:         raise WarehouseMapError("pallet_not_found")
1377:     from_label = (
1378:         await _location_label(session, pallet.storage_location_id)
1379:         if pallet.storage_location_id is not None
1380:         else UNASSIGNED_LABEL
1381:     )
1382:     code = pallet.code
1383:     try:
1384:         await pallet_service.disband_pallet(session, tenant_id, pallet_id)
1385:     except pallet_service.PalletServiceError as exc:
1386:         raise WarehouseMapError(exc.code) from exc
1387:     event = WarehouseMapEvent(
1388:         tenant_id=tenant_id,
1389:         warehouse_id=warehouse_id,
1390:         actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
1391:         subject=_container_title("pallet", code),
1392:         quantity=None,
1393:         from_label=from_label,
1394:         to_label=UNASSIGNED_LABEL,
1395:     )
1396:     session.add(event)
1397:     await session.commit()
1398:     return {"id": str(pallet_id), "disbanded": True}
1399: 
1400: 
1401: def _sorting_tree_rows(
1402:     nodes: list[dict[str, Any]],
1403:     *,
1404:     holder: str | None,
1405:     objects: list[dict[str, Any]],
1406:     lines: list[dict[str, Any]],
1407: ) -> None:
1408:     for node in nodes:
1409:         if node["kind"] == "product":
1410:             lines.append(
1411:                 {
1412:                     "id": node["id"],
1413:                     "productId": node["product_id"],
1414:                     "qty": node["qty"],
1415:                     "holder": holder,
1416:                 }
1417:             )
1418:             continue
1419:         object_holder = f"obj:{node['id']}"
1420:         objects.append(
1421:             {
1422:                 "id": node["id"],
1423:                 "kind": node["kind"],
1424:                 "code": node["code"],
1425:                 "barcode": node["barcode"] or "",
1426:                 "holder": holder,
1427:             }
1428:         )
1429:         _sorting_tree_rows(node["children"], holder=object_holder, objects=objects, lines=lines)
1430: 
1431: 
1432: async def _filter_sorting_map_by_inbound_request(
1433:     session: AsyncSession,
1434:     *,
1435:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1436:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
1437:     inbound_request_id: uuid.UUID,
1438:     data: dict[str, Any],
1439: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
1440:     request = await session.get(InboundIntakeRequest, inbound_request_id)
1441:     if (
1442:         request is None
1443:         or request.tenant_id != tenant_id
1444:         or request.warehouse_id != warehouse_id
1445:     ):
1446:         raise WarehouseMapError("inbound_request_not_found")
1447: 
1448:     accepted_rows = list(
1449:         (
1450:             await session.execute(
1451:                 select(InboundIntakeLine.product_id, InboundIntakeLine.actual_qty).where(
1452:                     InboundIntakeLine.request_id == inbound_request_id,
1453:                 )
1454:             )
1455:         ).all()
1456:     )
1457:     remaining_by_product = {
1458:         str(product_id): max(0, int(actual_qty or 0))
1459:         for product_id, actual_qty in accepted_rows
1460:     }
1461: 
1462:     box_rows = list(
1463:         (
1464:             await session.execute(
1465:                 select(InboundIntakeBox.id, InboundIntakeBox.pallet_id).where(
1466:                     InboundIntakeBox.request_id == inbound_request_id,
1467:                     InboundIntakeBox.tenant_id == tenant_id,
1468:                 )
1469:             )
1470:         ).all()
1471:     )
1472:     cargo_place_rows = list(
1473:         (
1474:             await session.execute(
1475:                 select(
1476:                     InboundIntakeCargoPlace.id,
1477:                     InboundIntakeCargoPlace.pallet_id,
1478:                 ).where(
1479:                     InboundIntakeCargoPlace.request_id == inbound_request_id,
1480:                     InboundIntakeCargoPlace.tenant_id == tenant_id,
1481:                 )
1482:             )
1483:         ).all()
1484:     )
1485:     allowed_containers = {
1486:         *(('box', str(container_id)) for container_id, _pallet_id in box_rows),
1487:         *(
1488:             ('cargo_place', str(container_id))
1489:             for container_id, _pallet_id in cargo_place_rows
1490:         ),
1491:         *(
1492:             ('pallet', str(pallet_id))
1493:             for _container_id, pallet_id in [*box_rows, *cargo_place_rows]
1494:             if pallet_id is not None
1495:         ),
1496:     }
1497:     generic_pallet_ids = list(
1498:         (
1499:             await session.scalars(
1500:                 select(Pallet.id).where(
1501:                     Pallet.tenant_id == tenant_id,
1502:                     Pallet.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
1503:                     Pallet.inbound_request_id == inbound_request_id,
1504:                     Pallet.disbanded_at.is_(None),
1505:                 )
1506:             )
1507:         ).all()
1508:     )
1509:     generic_box_rows = list(
1510:         (
1511:             await session.execute(
1512:                 select(WarehouseBox.id, WarehouseBox.container_kind).where(
1513:                     WarehouseBox.tenant_id == tenant_id,
1514:                     WarehouseBox.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
1515:                     WarehouseBox.inbound_request_id == inbound_request_id,
1516:                 )
1517:             )
1518:         ).all()
1519:     )
1520:     allowed_containers.update(
1521:         ("pallet", str(pallet_id)) for pallet_id in generic_pallet_ids
1522:     )
1523:     allowed_containers.update(
1524:         (container_kind, str(container_id))
1525:         for container_id, container_kind in generic_box_rows
1526:     )
1527: 
1528:     def filter_nodes(nodes: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
1529:         filtered: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
1530:         for node in nodes:
1531:             if node["kind"] == "product":
1532:                 product_id = node["product_id"]
1533:                 remaining = remaining_by_product.get(product_id, 0)
1534:                 quantity = min(int(node["qty"]), remaining)
1535:                 if quantity > 0:
1536:                     filtered.append({**node, "qty": quantity})
1537:                     remaining_by_product[product_id] = remaining - quantity
1538:                 continue
1539: 
1540:             children = filter_nodes(node["children"])
1541:             if (node["kind"], node["id"]) not in allowed_containers:
1542:                 # Тара другого документа не должна становиться владельцем товара
1543:                 # выбранной приёмки: относящиеся к документу строки поднимаем выше.
1544:                 filtered.extend(children)
1545:                 continue
1546:             filtered.append(
1547:                 _normalize_container(
1548:                     {
1549:                         **node,
1550:                         "children": children,
1551:                     }
1552:                 )
1553:             )
1554:         return filtered
1555: 
1556:     # Сначала расходуем документное количество в зоне сортировки. Это сохраняет
1557:     # только что принятую приёмку в «осталось поставить», даже если тот же SKU уже
1558:     # лежит в ячейках от прежних документов.
1559:     filtered_unassigned = filter_nodes(data["unassigned"])
1560:     filtered_cells = [
1561:         {
1562:             **cell,
1563:             "children": filter_nodes(cell["children"]),
1564:         }
1565:         for cell in data["cells"]
1566:     ]
1567:     return {
1568:         **data,
1569:         "unassigned": filtered_unassigned,
1570:         "cells": filtered_cells,
1571:     }
1572: 
1573: 
1574: async def get_sorting_objects(
1575:     session: AsyncSession,
1576:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1577:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
1578:     *,
1579:     inbound_request_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
1580: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
1581:     data = await get_warehouse_map(session, tenant_id, warehouse_id)
1582:     if inbound_request_id is not None:
1583:         data = await _filter_sorting_map_by_inbound_request(
1584:             session,
1585:             tenant_id=tenant_id,
1586:             warehouse_id=warehouse_id,
1587:             inbound_request_id=inbound_request_id,
1588:             data=data,
1589:         )
1590:     objects: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
1591:     lines: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
1592:     _sorting_tree_rows(data["unassigned"], holder=None, objects=objects, lines=lines)
1593:     cells: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
1594:     for row in data["cells"]:
1595:         cell_objects: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
1596:         cell_lines: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
1597:         _sorting_tree_rows(
1598:             row["children"],
1599:             holder=f"cell:{row['id']}",
1600:             objects=cell_objects,
1601:             lines=cell_lines,
1602:         )
1603:         objects.extend(cell_objects)
1604:         lines.extend(cell_lines)
1605:         cells.append(
1606:             {
1607:                 "id": row["id"],
1608:                 "code": row["code"],
1609:                 "barcode": row["barcode"],
1610:                 "objects": cell_objects,
1611:                 "lines": cell_lines,
1612:             }
1613:         )
1614:     product_ids = {uuid.UUID(row["productId"]) for row in lines}
1615:     products = (
1616:         list(
1617:             (
1618:                 await session.execute(
1619:                     select(Product, Seller)
1620:                     .outerjoin(Seller, Seller.id == Product.seller_id)
1621:                     .where(Product.tenant_id == tenant_id, Product.id.in_(product_ids))
1622:                 )
1623:             ).all()
1624:         )
1625:         if product_ids
1626:         else []
1627:     )
1628:     location_rows = (
1629:         list(
1630:             (
1631:                 await session.execute(
1632:                     select(
1633:                         InventoryBalance.product_id,
1634:                         StorageLocation,
1635:                         func.sum(InventoryBalance.quantity),
1636:                     )
1637:                     .join(StorageLocation)
1638:                     .where(
1639:                         InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
1640:                         InventoryBalance.product_id.in_(product_ids),
1641:                         InventoryBalance.quantity > 0,
1642:                         StorageLocation.warehouse_id == warehouse_id,
1643:                         StorageLocation.code != SORTING_LOCATION_CODE,
1644:                         StorageLocation.deleted_at.is_(None),
1645:                     )
1646:                     .group_by(InventoryBalance.product_id, StorageLocation.id)
1647:                 )
1648:             ).all()
1649:         )
1650:         if product_ids
1651:         else []
1652:     )
1653:     already: dict[uuid.UUID, dict[uuid.UUID, tuple[str, int]]] = defaultdict(dict)
1654:     for product_id, location, quantity in location_rows:
1655:         already[product_id][location.id] = (location.code, int(quantity or 0))
1656: 
1657:     product_nodes: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
1658: 
1659:     def collect_product_nodes(nodes: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
1660:         for node in nodes:
1661:             if node["kind"] == "product":
1662:                 product_nodes[node["product_id"]] = node
1663:             else:
1664:                 collect_product_nodes(node["children"])
1665: 
1666:     for cell in data["cells"]:
1667:         collect_product_nodes(cell["children"])
1668:     collect_product_nodes(data["unassigned"])
1669:     product_data = [
1670:         {
1671:             "id": str(product.id),
1672:             "name": product.name,
1673:             "sku": product.sku_code,
1674:             "seller": seller.name if seller is not None else "",
1675:             "barcode": product.wb_barcode or "",
1676:             "photo": product_nodes.get(str(product.id), {}).get("photo_url") or "",
1677:             "size": product.wb_size,
1678:             "alreadyAt": [
1679:                 {"cellId": str(cell_id), "code": code, "qty": qty}
1680:                 for cell_id, (code, qty) in already.get(product.id, {}).items()
1681:             ],
1682:         }
1683:         for product, seller in products
1684:     ]
1685:     return {
1686:         "objects": objects,
1687:         "lines": lines,
1688:         "products": product_data,
1689:         "cells": cells,
1690:     }
1691: 
1692: 
1693: async def _sorting_destination_kind(
1694:     session: AsyncSession,
1695:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1696:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
1697:     container_id: uuid.UUID,
1698: ) -> ContainerKind:
1699:     found: list[ContainerKind] = []
1700:     candidates: tuple[ContainerKind, ...] = ("pallet", "box", "cargo_place")
1701:     for candidate in candidates:
1702:         try:
1703:             await validate_container(
1704:                 session,
1705:                 tenant_id,
1706:                 warehouse_id,
1707:                 candidate,
1708:                 container_id,
1709:             )
1710:         except ValueError:
1711:             continue
1712:         found.append(candidate)
1713:     if len(found) != 1:
1714:         raise WarehouseMapError("destination_not_found")
1715:     return found[0]
1716: 
1717: 
1718: async def place_sorting_object(
1719:     session: AsyncSession,
1720:     *,
1721:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
1722:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID,
1723:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID,
1724:     kind: ObjectKind,
1725:     object_id: uuid.UUID,
1726:     cell_id: uuid.UUID | None,
1727:     to_id: uuid.UUID | None,
1728:     quantity: int | None,
1729: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
1730:     if cell_id is not None and to_id is not None:
1731:         raise WarehouseMapError("destination_conflict")
1732:     if cell_id is not None:
1733:         destination_kind: DestinationKind = "cell"
1734:         destination_id = cell_id
1735:     elif to_id is not None:
1736:         destination_kind = await _sorting_destination_kind(
1737:             session,
1738:             tenant_id,
1739:             warehouse_id,
1740:             to_id,
1741:         )
1742:         destination_id = to_id
1743:     else:
1744:         destination_kind = "unassigned"
1745:         destination_id = None
1746:     return await move_object(
1747:         session,
1748:         tenant_id=tenant_id,
1749:         warehouse_id=warehouse_id,
1750:         actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
1751:         kind=kind,
1752:         object_id=object_id,
1753:         to_kind=destination_kind,
1754:         to_id=destination_id,
1755:         quantity=quantity,
1756:     )
END_SOURCE

SOURCE wms062-warehouse-docs/backend/app/services/discrepancy_act_service.py SHA256=8a97509f385af70ee1a63a0ea57dbc67fe9bd64b3b33234474030ab6ffeafbe9 BASE_HEAD=ed930463ae3504e1eb1112031a98408a3ee227dd

18: STATUS_DRAFT = "draft"

24: class DiscrepancyActError(Exception):
25:     def __init__(self, code: str) -> None:
26:         self.code = code
27:         super().__init__(code)

30: async def create_act(
31:     session: AsyncSession,
32:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
33:     *,
34:     inbound_intake_request_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
35:     seller_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
36: ) -> DiscrepancyAct:
37:     resolved_seller_id = seller_id
38:     if inbound_intake_request_id is not None:
39:         inbound = await session.get(InboundIntakeRequest, inbound_intake_request_id)
40:         if inbound is None or inbound.tenant_id != tenant_id:
41:             raise DiscrepancyActError("inbound_not_found")
42:         if resolved_seller_id is None:
43:             resolved_seller_id = inbound.seller_id
44:     if resolved_seller_id is not None:
45:         sl = await session.get(Seller, resolved_seller_id)
46:         if sl is None or sl.tenant_id != tenant_id:
47:             raise DiscrepancyActError("seller_not_found")
48:     act = DiscrepancyAct(
49:         tenant_id=tenant_id,
50:         inbound_intake_request_id=inbound_intake_request_id,
51:         seller_id=resolved_seller_id,
52:         status=STATUS_DRAFT,
53:     )
54:     session.add(act)
55:     await session.commit()
56:     await session.refresh(act)
57:     return act

59: 
60: async def get_act(
61:     session: AsyncSession,
62:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
63:     act_id: uuid.UUID,
64:     *,
65:     lock: bool = False,
66: ) -> DiscrepancyAct | None:
67:     stmt = (
68:         select(DiscrepancyAct)
69:         .where(DiscrepancyAct.id == act_id)
70:         .where(DiscrepancyAct.tenant_id == tenant_id)
71:         .options(
72:             selectinload(DiscrepancyAct.inbound_intake_request),
73:             selectinload(DiscrepancyAct.seller),
74:             selectinload(DiscrepancyAct.lines).selectinload(DiscrepancyActLine.product),
75:         )
76:     )
77:     if lock:
78:         stmt = stmt.with_for_update().execution_options(populate_existing=True)
79:     res = await session.execute(stmt)
80:     return res.scalar_one_or_none()
81: 
82: 
83: async def list_acts(session: AsyncSession, tenant_id: uuid.UUID) -> list[DiscrepancyAct]:
84:     stmt = (
85:         select(DiscrepancyAct)
86:         .where(DiscrepancyAct.tenant_id == tenant_id)
87:         .options(
88:             selectinload(DiscrepancyAct.inbound_intake_request),
89:             selectinload(DiscrepancyAct.seller),
90:             selectinload(DiscrepancyAct.lines),
91:         )
92:         .order_by(DiscrepancyAct.created_at.desc())
93:     )
94:     res = await session.execute(stmt)
95:     return list(res.scalars().unique().all())

98: async def add_line(
99:     session: AsyncSession,
100:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
101:     act_id: uuid.UUID,
102:     *,
103:     product_id: uuid.UUID,
104:     quantity: int,
105:     inbound_intake_line_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
106: ) -> DiscrepancyActLine:
107:     act = await get_act(session, tenant_id, act_id, lock=True)
108:     if act is None:
109:         raise DiscrepancyActError("not_found")
110:     if act.status != STATUS_DRAFT:
111:         raise DiscrepancyActError("not_editable")
112:     if quantity == 0:
113:         raise DiscrepancyActError("invalid_quantity")
114:     prod = await session.get(Product, product_id)
115:     if prod is None or prod.tenant_id != tenant_id:
116:         raise DiscrepancyActError("product_not_found")
117:     if inbound_intake_line_id is not None:
118:         if act.inbound_intake_request_id is None:
119:             raise DiscrepancyActError("inbound_link_required")
120:         il = await session.get(InboundIntakeLine, inbound_intake_line_id)
121:         if il is None or il.request_id != act.inbound_intake_request_id:
122:             raise DiscrepancyActError("inbound_line_not_found")
123:         if il.product_id != product_id:
124:             raise DiscrepancyActError("product_mismatch")
125:     line = DiscrepancyActLine(
126:         act_id=act.id,
127:         product_id=product_id,
128:         quantity=quantity,
129:         inbound_intake_line_id=inbound_intake_line_id,
130:     )
131:     session.add(line)
132:     try:
133:         await session.commit()
134:     except IntegrityError:
135:         await session.rollback()
136:         raise DiscrepancyActError("duplicate_line") from None
137:     await session.refresh(line, attribute_names=["product"])
138:     return line
139: 
140: 
141: async def submit_act(
142:     session: AsyncSession,
143:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
144:     act_id: uuid.UUID,
145: ) -> DiscrepancyAct:
146:     act = await get_act(session, tenant_id, act_id, lock=True)
147:     if act is None:
148:         raise DiscrepancyActError("not_found")
149:     if act.status != STATUS_DRAFT:
150:         raise DiscrepancyActError("bad_status")
151:     if not act.lines:
152:         raise DiscrepancyActError("empty_act")
153:     act.status = STATUS_CONFIRMED
154:     await session.commit()
155:     r2 = await get_act(session, tenant_id, act_id)
156:     assert r2 is not None
157:     return r2
158: 
159: 
160: async def approve_act(
161:     session: AsyncSession,
162:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
163:     act_id: uuid.UUID,
164:     *,
165:     actor_user_id: uuid.UUID | None,
166: ) -> DiscrepancyAct:
167:     act = await get_act(session, tenant_id, act_id, lock=True)
168:     if act is None:
169:         raise DiscrepancyActError("not_found")
170:     if act.status != STATUS_CONFIRMED:
171:         raise DiscrepancyActError("bad_status")
172:     if act.inbound_intake_request is None:
173:         raise DiscrepancyActError("inbound_link_required")
174:     if not act.lines:
175:         raise DiscrepancyActError("empty_act")
176: 
177:     sorting_loc = await sorting_loc_svc.get_or_create_sorting_location(
178:         session,
179:         tenant_id,
180:         act.inbound_intake_request.warehouse_id,
181:     )
182:     for product_id in sorted({line.product_id for line in act.lines}, key=str):
183:         await inv_svc.lock_stock_product(session, tenant_id, product_id)
184:     # Balances may have been loaded before waiting for a concurrent stock writer.
185:     from app.models.inventory_balance import InventoryBalance
186: 
187:     (await session.scalars(
188:         select(InventoryBalance)
189:         .where(InventoryBalance.tenant_id == tenant_id,
190:                InventoryBalance.product_id.in_([line.product_id for line in act.lines]))
191:         .execution_options(populate_existing=True)
192:     )).all()
193:     for line in act.lines:
194:         if line.quantity == 0:
195:             raise DiscrepancyActError("invalid_quantity")
196:         try:
197:             await inv_svc.record_movement_and_adjust_balance(
198:                 session,
199:                 tenant_id=tenant_id,
200:                 product_id=line.product_id,
201:                 storage_location_id=sorting_loc.id,
202:                 quantity_delta=line.quantity,
203:                 movement_type=MOVEMENT_TYPE_DISCREPANCY_ACT,
204:                 inbound_intake_line_id=line.inbound_intake_line_id,
205:                 actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
206:             )
207:         except ValueError as exc:
208:             if str(exc) == "insufficient stock":
209:                 await session.rollback()
210:                 raise DiscrepancyActError("insufficient_stock") from exc
211:             raise
212:     act.status = STATUS_APPROVED
213:     await session.commit()
214:     r2 = await get_act(session, tenant_id, act_id)
215:     assert r2 is not None
216:     return r2
217: 
218: 
219: async def reject_act(
220:     session: AsyncSession,
221:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
222:     act_id: uuid.UUID,
223: ) -> DiscrepancyAct:
224:     act = await get_act(session, tenant_id, act_id, lock=True)
225:     if act is None:
226:         raise DiscrepancyActError("not_found")
227:     if act.status != STATUS_CONFIRMED:
228:         raise DiscrepancyActError("bad_status")
229:     act.status = STATUS_REJECTED
230:     await session.commit()
231:     r2 = await get_act(session, tenant_id, act_id)
232:     assert r2 is not None
233:     return r2
234: 
235: 
236: async def delete_line(
237:     session: AsyncSession,
238:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
239:     act_id: uuid.UUID,
240:     line_id: uuid.UUID,
241: ) -> None:
242:     act = await get_act(session, tenant_id, act_id, lock=True)
243:     if act is None:
244:         raise DiscrepancyActError("not_found")
245:     if act.status != STATUS_DRAFT:
246:         raise DiscrepancyActError("not_editable")
247:     line = await session.get(DiscrepancyActLine, line_id)
248:     if line is None or line.act_id != act_id:
249:         raise DiscrepancyActError("line_not_found")
250:     await session.delete(line)
251:     await session.commit()

79: class InventoryCountFoundIn(BaseModel):

81: 
82:     # Экран присылает все прочтения кода: как пришло со сканера и как это
83:     # выглядело бы в латинской раскладке. Иначе товар, который экран только что
84:     # нашёл переводом раскладки, сервер не находил, и оператор видел зелёное
85:     # «записываем» рядом с красным «товар не найден».
86:     barcodes: list[str] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=4)

88:     # ничего. Адрес из этого выводит сервер — палета может стоять без ячейки, и
89:     # знать об этом карточке тары, а не экрану.

220:     # В дерево они не попадают, иначе документ распухнет пустыми строками.
221:     scannable_cells: list[CountScannableCellOut] = []
222:     # Тара, выброшенная из дерева как пустая. Сканер обязан её открывать: пустой
223:     # по учёту короб — самый частый адресат находки («тут лежит то, чего по
224:     # бумагам здесь нет»). Пустые строки в дереве оператору не нужны, а пикнуть
225:     # такую тару он должен.
226:     scannable_containers: list[CountScannableContainerOut] = []
227: 
228: 
229: class ChangedBalanceOut(BaseModel):
230:     line_id: str
231:     product_id: str
232:     storage_location_id: str | None
233:     expected_quantity: int
234:     current_quantity: int
235:

238:     product_id: str
239:     product_name: str
240:     marketplace: str | None = None
241:     warehouse_id: str | None = None
242:     quantity: int
