245: class InventoryCountPostOut(BaseModel):
246:     id: str
247:     status: str
248:     posted_lines: int
249:     unchanged_lines: int
250:     changed_balance_count: int
251:     changed_balances: list[ChangedBalanceOut]
252:     stock_write_off: list[InventoryStockWriteOffOut] = Field(default_factory=list)
253: 
254: 
255: def _number(count: InventoryCount) -> str:
256:     return f"ИНВ-{str(count.id).split('-')[0].upper()}"
257: 
258: 
259: def _fill(count: InventoryCount) -> tuple[CountFillOut, str]:
260:     if count.source == service.SOURCE_OBJECT:
261:         return CountFillOut(mode="object", object_label="По объекту"), "По объекту"
262:     if count.seller_id is None and count.category is None:
263:         return CountFillOut(mode="all"), "Товары документа"
264:     parts = [count.seller.name if count.seller is not None else None, count.category]
265:     label = ", ".join(part for part in parts if part)
266:     return (
267:         CountFillOut(
268:             mode="filters",
269:             seller_id=str(count.seller_id) if count.seller_id is not None else None,
270:             category=count.category,
271:         ),
272:         label or "По фильтрам",
273:     )
274: 
275: 
276: async def _categories(
277:     session: AsyncSession,
278:     count: InventoryCount,
279: ) -> dict[uuid.UUID, str | None]:
280:     unique = {line.product.id: line.product for line in count.lines}
281:     return await service.product_categories(session, list(unique.values()))
282: 
283: 
284: async def _photos(
285:     session: AsyncSession,
286:     count: InventoryCount,
287: ) -> dict[uuid.UUID, str | None]:
288:     unique = {line.product.id: line.product for line in count.lines}
289:     return await service.product_photos(session, list(unique.values()))
290: 
291: 
292: async def _summary_out(
293:     session: AsyncSession,
294:     count: InventoryCount,
295: ) -> InventoryCountSummaryOut:
296:     current = await service.current_quantities(session, count)
297:     counted = 0
298:     discrepancies = 0
299:     surplus = 0
300:     shortage = 0
301:     for line in count.lines:
302:         if line.actual_quantity is None:
303:             continue
304:         counted += 1
305:         delta = int(line.actual_quantity) - current[line.id]
306:         if delta == 0:
307:             continue
308:         discrepancies += 1
309:         if delta > 0:
310:             surplus += delta
311:         else:
312:             shortage += -delta
313:     fill, fill_label = _fill(count)
314:     return InventoryCountSummaryOut(
315:         id=str(count.id),
316:         number=_number(count),
317:         status=count.status,
318:         source=count.source,
319:         warehouse_id=str(count.warehouse_id) if count.warehouse_id is not None else None,
320:         warehouse_name=count.warehouse.name if count.warehouse is not None else "Все склады",
321:         seller_id=str(count.seller_id) if count.seller_id is not None else None,
322:         category=count.category,
323:         fill=fill,
324:         fill_label=fill_label,
325:         created_at=count.created_at.isoformat(),
326:         created_by=count.created_by.email,
327:         lines=len(count.lines),
328:         counted=counted,
329:         discrepancies=discrepancies,
330:         surplus=surplus,
331:         shortage=shortage,
332:     )
333: 
334: 
335: def _product_node(
336:     line: InventoryCountLine,
337:     *,
338:     category: str | None,
339:     photo_url: str | None,
340:     current_quantity: int,
341: ) -> CountProductNodeOut:
342:     product = line.product
343:     expected_now = current_quantity if current_quantity != line.expected_quantity else None
344:     return CountProductNodeOut(
345:         id=str(line.id),
346:         product_id=str(product.id),
347:         name=product.name,
348:         sku=product.sku_code,
349:         seller=product.seller.name if product.seller is not None else "Без селлера",
350:         seller_id=str(product.seller_id) if product.seller_id is not None else None,
351:         category=category,
352:         barcode=product.wb_barcode,
353:         wb_vendor_code=product.wb_vendor_code,
354:         wb_barcode=product.wb_barcode,
355:         wb_size=product.wb_size,
356:         photo_url=photo_url,
357:         expected=int(line.expected_quantity),
358:         actual=int(line.actual_quantity) if line.actual_quantity is not None else None,
359:         expected_now=expected_now,
360:     )
361: 
362: 
363: def _container_tree(
364:     rows: list[dict[str, Any]],
365:     containers: dict[tuple[str, str], CountContainerNodeOut],
366:     cell_id: str | None,
367:     cell_of: dict[str, str | None],
368: ) -> list[CountProductNodeOut | CountContainerNodeOut]:
369:     result: list[CountProductNodeOut | CountContainerNodeOut] = []
370:     for row in rows:
371:         kind = row.get("kind")
372:         if kind not in {"pallet", "box", "cargo_place"}:
373:             continue
374:         node = CountContainerNodeOut(
375:             kind=kind,
376:             id=str(row["id"]),
377:             code=str(row["code"]),
378:             barcode=str(row["barcode"]) if row.get("barcode") is not None else None,
379:             children=_container_tree(row.get("children", []), containers, cell_id, cell_of),
380:         )
381:         containers[(kind, node.id)] = node
382:         cell_of[node.id] = cell_id
383:         result.append(node)
384:     return result
385: 
386: 
387: def _prune_empty_containers(
388:     nodes: list[CountProductNodeOut | CountContainerNodeOut],
389:     cell_of: dict[str, str | None],
390:     dropped: list[CountScannableContainerOut],
391:     protected: set[tuple[str, str]],
392: ) -> list[CountProductNodeOut | CountContainerNodeOut]:
393:     """Убрать из дерева тару, в которой по документу ничего не лежит.
394: 
395:     Карта склада отдаёт всю тару склада, а документ может быть по одному
396:     селлеру или по одной категории. В пересчёте «Империи ФФ» из 420 коробов
397:     товар лежал в 113, а остальные 307 висели строками «0 из 0»: документ
398:     вырастал до сорока тысяч пикселей, и найти в нём свой короб глазами было
399:     нельзя. Выброшенная тара не пропадает — она уезжает в `scannable_containers`
400:     и по-прежнему открывается сканом, чтобы записать в неё находку.
401: 
402:     Исключение — тара из `protected` (пары «вид, id»): она заведена прямо в
403:     этом документе кнопкой «Создать короб» и пуста по определению. Оператор
404:     её только что создал и должен видеть, куда класть товар, поэтому общее
405:     правило для неё не действует.
406:     """
407: 
408:     kept: list[CountProductNodeOut | CountContainerNodeOut] = []
409:     for node in nodes:
410:         if isinstance(node, CountProductNodeOut):
411:             kept.append(node)
412:             continue
413:         node.children = _prune_empty_containers(node.children, cell_of, dropped, protected)
414:         if node.children or (node.kind, node.id) in protected:
415:             kept.append(node)
416:             continue
417:         dropped.append(
418:             CountScannableContainerOut(
419:                 kind=node.kind,
420:                 id=node.id,
421:                 code=node.code,
422:                 barcode=node.barcode,
423:                 cell_id=cell_of.get(node.id),
424:             )
425:         )
426:     return kept
427: 
428: 
429: async def _detail_out(
430:     session: AsyncSession,
431:     count: InventoryCount,
432: ) -> InventoryCountDetailOut:
433:     address_storage = await tenant_settings_service.is_address_storage_enabled(
434:         session, count.tenant_id
435:     )
436:     current = await service.current_quantities(session, count)
437:     categories = await _categories(session, count)
438:     photos = await _photos(session, count)
439:     line_rows: list[InventoryCountLineOut] = []
440:     # Пустой список — нормальное значение: без адресного хранения ячеек нет.
441:     scannable_cells: list[CountScannableCellOut] = []
442:     nodes_by_cell: dict[
443:         tuple[str, str], list[CountProductNodeOut | CountContainerNodeOut]
444:     ] = defaultdict(list)
445:     no_address_nodes: list[CountProductNodeOut | CountContainerNodeOut] = []
446:     containers: dict[tuple[str, str], CountContainerNodeOut] = {}
447:     # В какой ячейке стоит тара: нужно, чтобы выброшенная из дерева пустая тара
448:     # знала свой адрес и находка легла туда же, куда легла бы по дереву.
449:     cell_of_container: dict[str, str | None] = {}
450:     cells_by_id: dict[str, CountCellOut] = {}
451:     unassigned: CountCellOut | None = None
452:     if count.warehouse_id is not None:
453:         warehouse_map = await warehouse_map_service.get_warehouse_map(
454:             session,
455:             count.tenant_id,
456:             count.warehouse_id,
457:         )
458:         unassigned = CountCellOut(
459:             id="unassigned",
460:             label=UNASSIGNED_LABEL if address_storage else "",
461:             barcode=None,
462:             children=_container_tree(
463:                 warehouse_map["unassigned"], containers, None, cell_of_container
464:             ),
465:         )
466:         for map_cell in warehouse_map["cells"]:
467:             cell = CountCellOut(
468:                 id=str(map_cell["id"]),
469:                 label=str(map_cell["code"]),
470:                 barcode=(
471:                     str(map_cell["barcode"])
472:                     if map_cell.get("barcode") is not None
473:                     else None
474:                 ),
475:                 children=_container_tree(
476:                     map_cell["children"],
477:                     containers,
478:                     str(map_cell["id"]),
479:                     cell_of_container,
480:                 ),
481:             )
482:             cells_by_id[cell.id] = cell
483:     for line in count.lines:
484:         product = line.product
485:         location = line.storage_location
486:         category = categories.get(product.id)
487:         node = _product_node(
488:             line,
489:             category=category,
490:             photo_url=photos.get(product.id),
491:             current_quantity=current[line.id],
492:         )
493:         container = (
494:             containers.get((line.container_kind, str(line.container_id)))
495:             if line.container_kind is not None and line.container_id is not None
496:             else None
497:         )
498:         if container is not None:
499:             container.children.append(node)
500:         elif address_storage and location is not None:
501:             label = (
502:                 UNASSIGNED_LABEL
503:                 if location.code == SORTING_LOCATION_CODE
504:                 else location.code
505:             )
506:             if location.code == SORTING_LOCATION_CODE and unassigned is not None:
507:                 unassigned.children.append(node)
508:             elif str(location.id) in cells_by_id:
509:                 cells_by_id[str(location.id)].children.append(node)
510:             else:
511:                 nodes_by_cell[(str(location.id), label)].append(node)
512:         else:
513:             if unassigned is not None:
514:                 unassigned.children.append(node)
515:             else:
516:                 no_address_nodes.append(node)
517:         line_rows.append(
518:             InventoryCountLineOut(
519:                 id=str(line.id),
520:                 product_id=str(line.product_id),
521:                 storage_location_id=(
522:                     str(line.storage_location_id)
523:                     if address_storage and line.storage_location_id is not None
524:                     else None
525:                 ),
526:                 storage_location_code=(
527:                     location.code if address_storage and location is not None else None
528:                 ),
529:                 container_kind=line.container_kind,
530:                 container_id=str(line.container_id) if line.container_id is not None else None,
531:                 product_name=product.name,
532:                 sku_code=product.sku_code,
533:                 seller_id=str(product.seller_id) if product.seller_id is not None else None,
534:                 seller_name=product.seller.name if product.seller is not None else None,
535:                 category=category,
536:                 barcode=product.wb_barcode,
537:                 wb_vendor_code=product.wb_vendor_code,
538:                 wb_barcode=product.wb_barcode,
539:                 wb_size=product.wb_size,
540:                 expected_quantity=int(line.expected_quantity),
541:                 current_quantity=current[line.id],
542:                 actual_quantity=(
543:                     int(line.actual_quantity) if line.actual_quantity is not None else None
544:                 ),
545:                 posted_delta=int(line.posted_delta) if line.posted_delta is not None else None,
546:                 balance_changed=current[line.id] != line.expected_quantity,
547:             )
548:         )
549:     # Тара без строк документа уезжает из дерева в список сканируемой: пикнуть
550:     # её по-прежнему можно, а пустой строкой она документ не раздувает. Тара,
551:     # заведённая прямо в этом документе, — исключение, см. _prune_empty_containers.
552:     created_container_ids = await service.created_container_ids(session, count.id)
553:     created_container_ids.update((item["kind"], item["id"]) for item in count.empty_containers)
554:     scannable_containers: list[CountScannableContainerOut] = []
555:     if unassigned is not None:
556:         unassigned.children = _prune_empty_containers(
557:             unassigned.children, cell_of_container, scannable_containers, created_container_ids
558:         )
559:     for cell in cells_by_id.values():
560:         cell.children = _prune_empty_containers(
561:             cell.children, cell_of_container, scannable_containers, created_container_ids
562:         )
563: 
564:     if address_storage:
565:         fallback_cells = [
566:             CountCellOut(id=cell_id, label=label, barcode=None, children=children)
567:             for (cell_id, label), children in sorted(
568:                 nodes_by_cell.items(), key=lambda item: item[0][1]
569:             )
570:         ]
571:         cells = [
572:             *([unassigned] if unassigned is not None and unassigned.children else []),
573:             *[
574:                 cell
575:                 for cell in cells_by_id.values()
576:                 if cell.children
577:             ],
578:             *fallback_cells,
579:         ]
580:         # ⛔ Пустые по учёту ячейки в дерево не тащим — иначе документ по складу
581:         # распухнет сотнями пустых строк. Но сканер обязан их узнавать: «в
582:         # ячейке лежит то, чего по учёту тут нет» — это первый и главный случай,
583:         # ради которого пересчёт и делают. Раньше штрихкод такой ячейки сканер
584:         # не знал, уходил искать товар, не находил и предлагал записать находку
585:         # со штрихкодом ЯЧЕЙКИ вместо товара.
586:         scannable_cells = [
587:             CountScannableCellOut(id=cell.id, label=cell.label, barcode=cell.barcode)
588:             for cell in cells_by_id.values()
589:             if cell.barcode
590:         ]
591:     else:
592:         # Frontend flattens children when addressStorage=false and never renders
593:         # this technical wrapper as a cell.
594:         cells = [
595:             CountCellOut(
596:                 id="inventory",
597:                 label="",
598:                 barcode=None,
599:             children=(unassigned.children if unassigned is not None else no_address_nodes),
600:             )
601:         ]
602:     fill, _ = _fill(count)
603:     return InventoryCountDetailOut(
604:         id=str(count.id),
605:         number=_number(count),
606:         status=count.status,
607:         source=count.source,
608:         warehouse_id=str(count.warehouse_id) if count.warehouse_id is not None else None,
609:         warehouse_name=count.warehouse.name if count.warehouse is not None else "Все склады",
610:         seller_id=str(count.seller_id) if count.seller_id is not None else None,
611:         category=count.category,
612:         fill=fill,
613:         created_at=count.created_at.isoformat(),
614:         created_by=count.created_by.email,
615:         posted_at=count.posted_at.isoformat() if count.posted_at is not None else None,
616:         posted_by=count.posted_by.email if count.posted_by is not None else None,
617:         comment=count.comment or "",
618:         empty_containers=count.empty_containers,
619:         address_storage=address_storage,
620:         lines=line_rows,
621:         cells=cells,
622:         scannable_cells=scannable_cells,
623:         scannable_containers=scannable_containers,
624:     )
625: 
626: 
627: def _http_error(exc: service.InventoryCountError) -> HTTPException:
628:     if exc.code in {
629:         "not_found",
630:         "count_not_found",
631:         "container_not_found",
632:         "warehouse_required_without_address_storage",
633:         "line_not_found",
634:         "seller_not_found",
635:         "warehouse_not_found",
636:         "object_not_found",
637:         "product_not_found",
638:         "storage_location_not_found",
639:         "location_not_found",
640:     }:
641:         return HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail=exc.code)
642:     if exc.code in {
643:         "not_editable",
644:         "already_posted",
645:         "not_cancellable",
646:         "empty_count",
647:         "comment_changed",
648:         "container_has_no_stock",
649:         "balance_changed_during_post",
650:         "count_not_editable",
651:         "barcode_is_ambiguous",
652:         "container_reference_invalid",
653:         # Переложить товар в тару (WMS-153): состояние на момент действия не
654:         # позволяет продолжить, а не «данных не существует».
655:         "already_there",
656:         "product_already_at_destination",
657:         "move_source_empty",
658:         # Удалить пустую тару (WMS-153).
659:         "container_not_empty",
660:         "container_linked_to_inbound",
661:     }:
662:         return HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT, detail=exc.code)
663:     return HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT, detail=exc.code)
664: 
665: 
666: @router.get("", response_model=list[InventoryCountSummaryOut])
667: async def list_inventory_counts(
668:     user: Annotated[User, Depends(require_inventory_access)],
669:     session: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],
670:     count_status: Annotated[str | None, Query(alias="status")] = None,
671:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
672:     seller_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
673:     created_from: datetime | None = None,
674:     created_to: datetime | None = None,
675: ) -> list[InventoryCountSummaryOut]:
676:     rows = await service.list_counts(
677:         session,
678:         user.tenant_id,
679:         status=count_status,
680:         warehouse_id=warehouse_id,
681:         seller_id=seller_id,
682:         created_from=created_from,
683:         created_to=created_to,
684:     )
685:     return [await _summary_out(session, count) for count in rows]
686: 
687: 
688: @router.post(
689:     "",
690:     response_model=InventoryCountDetailOut,
691:     status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
692: )
693: async def create_inventory_count(
694:     body: InventoryCountCreateIn,
695:     user: Annotated[User, Depends(require_inventory_access)],
696:     session: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],
697: ) -> InventoryCountDetailOut:
698:     object_scope = (
699:         service.CountObject(
700:             type=body.object.type,
701:             id=body.object.id,
702:             storage_location_id=body.object.storage_location_id,
703:         )
704:         if body.object is not None
705:         else None
706:     )
707:     filters = (
708:         service.CountFilters(
709:             seller_id=body.filters.seller_id,
710:             category=body.filters.category,
711:             warehouse_id=body.filters.warehouse_id,
712:             all=body.filters.all,
713:             product_ids=body.filters.product_ids,
714:         )
715:         if body.filters is not None
716:         else None
717:     )
718:     try:
719:         count = await service.create_count(
720:             session,
721:             user.tenant_id,
722:             user.id,
723:             source=body.source,
724:             object_scope=object_scope,
725:             filters=filters,
726:             comment=body.comment,
727:         )
728:     except service.InventoryCountError as exc:
729:         raise _http_error(exc) from None
730:     return await _detail_out(session, count)
731: 
732: 
733: @router.get("/{count_id}", response_model=InventoryCountDetailOut)
734: async def get_inventory_count(
735:     count_id: uuid.UUID,
736:     user: Annotated[User, Depends(require_inventory_access)],
737:     session: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],
738: ) -> InventoryCountDetailOut:
739:     count = await service.get_count(session, user.tenant_id, count_id)
740:     if count is None:
741:         raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="not_found")
742:     return await _detail_out(session, count)

744: 
745: @router.put("/{count_id}/lines", response_model=InventoryCountDetailOut)
746: async def save_inventory_count_lines(
747:     count_id: uuid.UUID,
748:     body: InventoryCountActualBatchIn,
749:     user: Annotated[User, Depends(require_inventory_access)],
750:     session: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],
751: ) -> InventoryCountDetailOut:
752:     try:
753:         if body.update_comment:
754:             count = await service.save_actuals(
755:                 session,
756:                 user.tenant_id,
757:                 count_id,
758:                 [(line.line_id, line.actual_quantity) for line in body.lines],
759:                 comment=body.comment,
760:                 expected_comment=(
761:                     body.expected_comment if "expected_comment" in body.model_fields_set
762:                     else service._SENTINEL
763:                 ),
764:             )
765:         else:
766:             count = await service.save_actuals(
767:                 session,
768:                 user.tenant_id,
769:                 count_id,
770:                 [(line.line_id, line.actual_quantity) for line in body.lines],
771:             )
772:     except service.InventoryCountError as exc:
773:         raise _http_error(exc) from None
774:     return await _detail_out(session, count)

983: 
984: @router.post("/{count_id}/post", response_model=InventoryCountPostOut)
985: async def post_inventory_count(
986:     count_id: uuid.UUID,
987:     user: Annotated[User, Depends(require_inventory_access)],
988:     session: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],
989: ) -> InventoryCountPostOut:
990:     try:
991:         result = await service.post_count(session, user.tenant_id, count_id, user.id)
992:     except service.InventoryCountError as exc:
993:         raise _http_error(exc) from None
994:     address_storage = await tenant_settings_service.is_address_storage_enabled(
995:         session, user.tenant_id
996:     )
997:     changed = [
998:         ChangedBalanceOut(
999:             line_id=str(item.line_id),
1000:             product_id=str(item.product_id),
1001:             storage_location_id=(
1002:                 str(item.storage_location_id)
1003:                 if address_storage and item.storage_location_id is not None
1004:                 else None
1005:             ),
1006:             expected_quantity=item.expected_quantity,
1007:             current_quantity=item.current_quantity,
1008:         )
1009:         for item in result.changed_balances
1010:     ]
1011:     totals: dict[tuple[uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID | None], int] = defaultdict(int)
1012:     for product_id, binding_id, quantity in result.stock_deductions:
1013:         totals[(product_id, binding_id)] += quantity
1014:     products = {
1015:         p.id: p.name
1016:         for p in (
1017:             await session.scalars(
1018:                 select(Product).where(
1019:                     Product.tenant_id == user.tenant_id, Product.id.in_({pid for pid, _ in totals})
1020:                 )
1021:             )
1022:         ).all()
1023:     }
1024:     bindings = {
1025:         b.id: b
1026:         for b in (
1027:             await session.scalars(
1028:                 select(FbsWarehouseBinding).where(
1029:                     FbsWarehouseBinding.tenant_id == user.tenant_id,
1030:                     FbsWarehouseBinding.id.in_({bid for _, bid in totals if bid is not None}),
1031:                 )
1032:             )
1033:         ).all()
1034:     }
1035:     write_off = []
1036:     for (product_id, binding_id), quantity in totals.items():
1037:         if quantity <= 0:
1038:             continue
1039:         binding = bindings.get(binding_id) if binding_id is not None else None
1040:         write_off.append(
1041:             InventoryStockWriteOffOut(
1042:                 product_id=str(product_id),
1043:                 product_name=products.get(product_id, str(product_id)),
1044:                 marketplace=binding.marketplace if binding is not None else None,
1045:                 warehouse_id=(binding.external_warehouse_id or str(binding.wb_warehouse_id))
1046:                 if binding is not None
1047:                 else None,
1048:                 quantity=quantity,
1049:             )
1050:         )
1051:     return InventoryCountPostOut(
1052:         id=str(result.count.id),
1053:         status=result.count.status,
1054:         posted_lines=result.posted_lines,
1055:         unchanged_lines=result.unchanged_lines,
1056:         changed_balance_count=len(changed),
1057:         changed_balances=changed,
1058:         stock_write_off=write_off,
1059:     )
1060: 
1061: 
1062: @router.delete("/{count_id}", response_model=InventoryCountDetailOut)
1063: async def cancel_inventory_count(
1064:     count_id: uuid.UUID,
1065:     user: Annotated[User, Depends(require_inventory_access)],
1066:     session: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],
1067: ) -> InventoryCountDetailOut:
1068:     try:
1069:         count = await service.cancel_count(session, user.tenant_id, count_id)
1070:     except service.InventoryCountError as exc:
1071:         raise _http_error(exc) from None
1072:     return await _detail_out(session, count)

149: class InventoryCountFoundScan(Base):
150:     """Один скан находки — чтобы повтор не превращался в лишнюю штуку.
151: 
152:     Кладовщик сканирует по складскому вайфаю, который рвётся. Ответ не доехал,
153:     экран показал ошибку, а сервер уже записал. Человек сканирует ещё раз —
154:     и на сервере становится две штуки вместо одной. Излишек на ровном месте, и
155:     найти его потом нечем: в документе просто стоит цифра.
156: 
157:     Клиент присылает идентификатор скана, мы его запоминаем и на повтор
158:     отвечаем тем же результатом, ничего не прибавляя.
159:     """
160: 
161:     __tablename__ = "inventory_count_found_scans"
162:     __table_args__ = (
163:         UniqueConstraint("count_id", "scan_id", name="uq_inventory_found_scan"),
164:     )
165: 
166:     id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
167:         Uuid(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4
168:     )
169:     tenant_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
170:         Uuid(as_uuid=True), nullable=False, index=True
171:     )
172:     count_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
173:         Uuid(as_uuid=True),
174:         ForeignKey("inventory_counts.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
175:         nullable=False,
176:     )
177:     line_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
178:         Uuid(as_uuid=True),
179:         ForeignKey("inventory_count_lines.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
180:         nullable=False,
181:     )
182:     scan_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
183:     expected_quantity: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)
184:     created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
185:         DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False, default=lambda: datetime.now(UTC)
186:     )
187: 
188: 
189: class InventoryCountCreatedContainer(Base):
190:     """Тара, заведённая прямо в документе пересчёта — исключение из прунинга.
191: 
192:     Карта склада отдаёт всю тару склада, и `_prune_empty_containers` в
193:     `app/api/inventory_counts.py` выбрасывает из дерева документа ту, в
194:     которой по документу ничего не лежит — иначе документ распухает сотнями
195:     строк «0 из 0» чужой тары (см. докстринг там же). Но короб, который
196:     оператор только что создал прямо в ЭТОМ документе, пуст по определению —
197:     и общее правило вышвыривало его сразу же, хотя человеку нужно видеть,
198:     куда класть товар. Эта таблица — точечный список исключений на пару
199:     (документ, тара), а не отключение прунинга.
200:     """
201: 
202:     __tablename__ = "inventory_count_created_containers"
203:     __table_args__ = (
204:         UniqueConstraint(
205:             "count_id",
206:             "container_kind",
207:             "container_id",
208:             name="uq_inventory_count_created_container",
209:         ),
210:     )
211: 
212:     id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
213:         Uuid(as_uuid=True), primary_key=True, default=uuid.uuid4
214:     )
215:     tenant_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
216:         Uuid(as_uuid=True), nullable=False, index=True
217:     )
218:     count_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(
219:         Uuid(as_uuid=True),
220:         ForeignKey("inventory_counts.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
221:         nullable=False,
222:         index=True,
223:     )
224:     container_kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
225:     container_id: Mapped[uuid.UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid(as_uuid=True), nullable=False)
226:     created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
227:         DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False, default=lambda: datetime.now(UTC)
228:     )
END_SOURCE

31: @dataclass(frozen=True)
32: class TenantSetup:
33:     headers: dict[str, str]
34:     tenant_id: uuid.UUID
35:     warehouse_id: uuid.UUID
36:     location_id: uuid.UUID

38: 
39: async def _tenant(async_client: AsyncClient, label: str) -> TenantSetup:
40:     suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]
41:     response = await async_client.post(
42:         "/auth/register",
43:         json={
44:             "organization_name": label,
45:             "slug": f"{label.lower()}-{suffix}",
46:             "admin_email": f"{label.lower()}-{suffix}@example.com",
47:             "password": "password123",
48:         },
49:     )
50:     assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
51:     headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {response.json()['access_token']}"}
52:     me = await async_client.get("/auth/me", headers=headers)
53:     warehouse = await async_client.post(
54:         "/warehouses",
55:         headers=headers,
56:         json={"name": f"Склад {label}", "code": f"WH-{suffix}"},
57:     )
58:     location = await async_client.post(
59:         f"/warehouses/{warehouse.json()['id']}/locations",
60:         headers=headers,
61:         json={"code": f"A-{suffix}"},
62:     )
63:     assert me.status_code == warehouse.status_code == location.status_code == 200
64:     return TenantSetup(
65:         headers=headers,
66:         tenant_id=uuid.UUID(me.json()["tenant_id"]),
67:         warehouse_id=uuid.UUID(warehouse.json()["id"]),
68:         location_id=uuid.UUID(location.json()["id"]),
69:     )
70: 
71: 
72: async def _seller(async_client: AsyncClient, setup: TenantSetup, name: str) -> uuid.UUID:
73:     response = await async_client.post(
74:         "/sellers", headers=setup.headers, json={"name": name}
75:     )
76:     assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
77:     return uuid.UUID(response.json()["id"])
78: 
79: 
80: async def _product(
81:     async_client: AsyncClient,
82:     setup: TenantSetup,
83:     *,
84:     name: str,
85:     seller_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
86: ) -> uuid.UUID:
87:     response = await async_client.post(
88:         "/products",
89:         headers=setup.headers,
90:         json={
91:             "name": name,
92:             "sku_code": f"SKU-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}",
93:             "seller_id": str(seller_id) if seller_id is not None else None,
94:             "length_mm": 1,
95:             "width_mm": 1,
96:             "height_mm": 1,
97:         },
98:     )
99:     assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
100:     return uuid.UUID(response.json()["id"])
101: 
102: 
103: async def _balance(
104:     setup: TenantSetup,
105:     product_id: uuid.UUID,
106:     quantity: int,
107:     *,
108:     location_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
109:     container_kind: str | None = None,
110:     container_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
111: ) -> None:
112:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
113:         session.add(
114:             InventoryBalance(
115:                 tenant_id=setup.tenant_id,
116:                 storage_location_id=location_id or setup.location_id,
117:                 product_id=product_id,
118:                 container_kind=container_kind,
119:                 container_id=container_id,
120:                 quantity=quantity,
121:                 quantity_unpacked=quantity,
122:                 quantity_packed=0,
123:             )
124:         )
125:         await session.commit()
126: 
127: 
128: async def _containers(
129:     setup: TenantSetup,
130: ) -> tuple[uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID, uuid.UUID]:
131:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
132:         request = InboundIntakeRequest(
133:             tenant_id=setup.tenant_id,
134:             warehouse_id=setup.warehouse_id,
135:             status="receiving",
136:         )
137:         pallet = Pallet(
138:             tenant_id=setup.tenant_id,
139:             warehouse_id=setup.warehouse_id,
140:             code=f"П-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}",
141:             barcode=f"PLT-{uuid.uuid4().hex}",
142:             storage_location_id=setup.location_id,
143:         )
144:         session.add_all([request, pallet])
145:         await session.flush()
146:         warehouse_box = WarehouseBox(
147:             tenant_id=setup.tenant_id,
148:             warehouse_id=setup.warehouse_id,
149:             internal_barcode=f"BOX-{uuid.uuid4().hex}",
150:             storage_location_id=setup.location_id,
151:             pallet_id=pallet.id,
152:         )
153:         empty_box = WarehouseBox(
154:             tenant_id=setup.tenant_id,
155:             warehouse_id=setup.warehouse_id,
156:             internal_barcode=f"EMPTY-{uuid.uuid4().hex}",
157:             storage_location_id=setup.location_id,
158:         )
159:         cargo_place = InboundIntakeCargoPlace(
160:             tenant_id=setup.tenant_id,
161:             request_id=request.id,
162:             place_number=1,
163:             internal_barcode=f"CARGO-{uuid.uuid4().hex}",
164:             pallet_id=pallet.id,
165:         )
166:         session.add_all([warehouse_box, empty_box, cargo_place])
167:         await session.commit()
168:         return pallet.id, warehouse_box.id, cargo_place.id, empty_box.id
169:

172:     response = await async_client.post(
173:         "/operations/inventory-counts",
174:         headers=setup.headers,
175:         json={
176:             "source": "planned",
177:             "filters": {"warehouse_id": str(setup.warehouse_id), "all": True},
178:         },
179:     )
180:     assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
181:     return response.json()

249: @pytest.mark.asyncio
250: async def test_inventory_count_selected_products_narrow_existing_scope(
251:     async_client: AsyncClient,
252: ) -> None:
253:     setup = await _tenant(async_client, "Selected")
254:     other = await _tenant(async_client, "OtherSelected")
255:     seller = await _seller(async_client, setup, "Селлер")
256:     first = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Первый", seller_id=seller)
257:     second = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Второй")
258:     third = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Третий")
259:     foreign = await _product(async_client, other, name="Чужой")
260:     _, box, _, _ = await _containers(setup)
261:     await _balance(setup, first, 5)
262:     await _balance(setup, first, 2, container_kind="box", container_id=box)
263:     await _balance(setup, second, -3)
264:     await _balance(setup, third, 9)
265:     await _balance(other, foreign, 12)
266:     warehouse = await async_client.post(
267:         "/warehouses", headers=setup.headers, json={"name": "Второй склад", "code": "WH2"}
268:     )
269:     location = await async_client.post(
270:         f"/warehouses/{warehouse.json()['id']}/locations",
271:         headers=setup.headers,
272:         json={"code": "B2"},
273:     )
274:     assert warehouse.status_code == location.status_code == 200
275:     await _balance(setup, first, 20, location_id=uuid.UUID(location.json()["id"]))
276:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
277:         product = await session.get(Product, first)
278:         assert product is not None
279:         product.category = "Одежда"
280:         await session.commit()
281: 
282:     async def create(filters: dict[str, object]) -> dict:
283:         response = await async_client.post(
284:             "/operations/inventory-counts",
285:             headers=setup.headers,
286:             json={
287:                 "source": "planned",
288:                 "filters": {"warehouse_id": str(setup.warehouse_id), **filters},
289:                 "comment": "Точечный пересчёт",
290:             },
291:         )
292:         assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
293:         return response.json()
294: 
295:     single = await create({"product_ids": [str(first), str(first)]})
296:     assert sorted(line["expected_quantity"] for line in single["lines"]) == [2, 5]
297:     assert {line["product_id"] for line in single["lines"]} == {str(first)}
298:     assert any(line["container_id"] == str(box) for line in single["lines"])
299:     assert single["comment"] == "Точечный пересчёт"
300:     reread = await async_client.get(
301:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{single['id']}", headers=setup.headers
302:     )
303:     assert reread.status_code == 200
304:     assert len(reread.json()["lines"]) == 2
305: 
306:     selected = [str(first), str(second), str(foreign), str(uuid.uuid4())]
307:     multiple = await create({"product_ids": selected})
308:     assert {line["product_id"] for line in multiple["lines"]} == {str(first), str(second)}
309:     assert sorted(line["expected_quantity"] for line in multiple["lines"]) == [-3, 2, 5]
310:     for narrowing in ({"seller_id": str(seller)}, {"category": "Одежда"}):
311:         narrowed = await create({"product_ids": selected, **narrowing})
312:         assert {line["product_id"] for line in narrowed["lines"]} == {str(first)}
313:     assert (await create({"product_ids": [str(foreign)]}))["lines"] == []
314:     for unfiltered in ({}, {"product_ids": []}, {"product_ids": None}):
315:         whole = await create(unfiltered)
316:         assert {line["product_id"] for line in whole["lines"]} == {
317:             str(first), str(second), str(third)
318:         }

352: @pytest.mark.asyncio
353: async def test_inventory_count_uses_human_label_for_sorting_location(
354:     async_client: AsyncClient,
355: ) -> None:
356:     # TC-NEW-INVENTORY-SORTING-LABEL-001
357:     # Дано: товар ещё лежит в системной sorting-зоне. Когда оператор открывает
358:     # документ пересчёта, тогда заголовок группы говорит «Без ячеек», а не
359:     # раскрывает технический код, по которому backend находит эту зону.
360:     setup = await _tenant(async_client, "SortingLabel")
361:     product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Товар без ячейки")
362:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
363:         sorting = await get_or_create_sorting_location(
364:             session, setup.tenant_id, setup.warehouse_id
365:         )
366:         sorting_id = sorting.id
367:         await session.commit()
368:     await _balance(setup, product, 4, location_id=sorting_id)
369: 
370:     response = await async_client.post(
371:         "/operations/inventory-counts",
372:         headers=setup.headers,
373:         json={
374:             "source": "object",
375:             "object": {"type": "product", "id": str(product)},
376:         },
377:     )
378: 
379:     assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
380:     cells = response.json()["cells"]
381:     assert cells[0]["label"] == UNASSIGNED_LABEL
382:     assert cells[0]["label"] != SORTING_LOCATION_CODE
383:

385: @pytest.mark.asyncio
386: async def test_inventory_count_by_box_uses_exact_box_balances(
387:     async_client: AsyncClient,
388: ) -> None:
389:     setup = await _tenant(async_client, "BoxCount")
390:     first_product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Товар 18")
391:     second_product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Товар 7")
392:     _pallet_id, box_id, _cargo_place_id, _empty_box_id = await _containers(setup)
393:     await _balance(
394:         setup,
395:         first_product,
396:         18,
397:         container_kind="box",
398:         container_id=box_id,
399:     )
400:     await _balance(
401:         setup,
402:         second_product,
403:         7,
404:         container_kind="box",
405:         container_id=box_id,
406:     )
407:     # Тот же SKU лежит россыпью в той же ячейке. Пересчёт короба не должен
408:     # смешивать этот остаток с 18 штуками внутри короба.
409:     await _balance(setup, first_product, 99)
410: 
411:     response = await async_client.post(
412:         "/operations/inventory-counts",
413:         headers=setup.headers,
414:         json={
415:             "source": "object",
416:             "object": {"type": "box", "id": str(box_id)},
417:         },
418:     )
419: 
420:     assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
421:     lines = {
422:         line["product_id"]: (
423:             line["expected_quantity"],
424:             line["container_kind"],
425:             line["container_id"],
426:         )
427:         for line in response.json()["lines"]
428:     }
429:     assert lines == {
430:         str(first_product): (18, "box", str(box_id)),
431:         str(second_product): (7, "box", str(box_id)),
432:     }

572: @pytest.mark.asyncio
573: async def test_inventory_count_zero_blank_match_and_mismatch_create_exact_movements(
574:     async_client: AsyncClient,
575: ) -> None:
576:     setup = await _tenant(async_client, "Posting")
577:     zero_product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Насчитали ноль")
578:     blank_product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Не считали")
579:     match_product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Сошлось")
580:     await _balance(setup, zero_product, 5)
581:     await _balance(setup, blank_product, 4)
582:     await _balance(setup, match_product, 3)
583:     count = await _create_all(async_client, setup)
584:     lines = {line["product_id"]: line for line in count["lines"]}
585: 
586:     saved = await async_client.put(
587:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}/lines",
588:         headers=setup.headers,
589:         json={
590:             "lines": [
591:                 {"line_id": lines[str(zero_product)]["id"], "actual_quantity": 0},
592:                 {"line_id": lines[str(match_product)]["id"], "actual_quantity": 3},
593:             ]
594:         },
595:     )
596:     assert saved.status_code == 200, saved.text
597:     saved_lines = {line["product_id"]: line for line in saved.json()["lines"]}
598:     assert saved_lines[str(zero_product)]["actual_quantity"] == 0
599:     assert saved_lines[str(blank_product)]["actual_quantity"] is None
600: 
601:     posted = await async_client.post(
602:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}/post", headers=setup.headers
603:     )
604:     assert posted.status_code == 200, posted.text
605:     assert posted.json()["posted_lines"] == 1
606:     assert posted.json()["unchanged_lines"] == 1
607: 
608:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
609:         balances = {
610:             product_id: quantity
611:             for product_id, quantity in (
612:                 await session.execute(
613:                     select(InventoryBalance.product_id, InventoryBalance.quantity).where(
614:                         InventoryBalance.tenant_id == setup.tenant_id
615:                     )
616:                 )
617:             ).all()
618:         }
619:         movements = list(
620:             (
621:                 await session.execute(
622:                     select(InventoryMovement).where(
623:                         InventoryMovement.movement_type == "inventory_count"
624:                     )
625:                 )
626:             ).scalars()
627:         )
628:     assert balances[zero_product] == 0
629:     assert balances[blank_product] == 4
630:     assert balances[match_product] == 3
631:     assert len(movements) == 1
632:     assert movements[0].product_id == zero_product
633:     assert movements[0].quantity_delta == -5
634:     assert movements[0].inventory_count_line_id == uuid.UUID(
635:         lines[str(zero_product)]["id"]
636:     )
637: 
638:     repeated = await async_client.post(
639:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}/post", headers=setup.headers
640:     )
641:     edited = await async_client.put(
642:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}/lines",
643:         headers=setup.headers,
644:         json={
645:             "lines": [
646:                 {"line_id": lines[str(zero_product)]["id"], "actual_quantity": 1}
647:             ]
648:         },
649:     )
650:     assert repeated.status_code == 409
651:     assert repeated.json()["detail"] == "already_posted"
652:     assert edited.status_code == 409
653:     assert edited.json()["detail"] == "not_editable"
654:

658:     async_client: AsyncClient,

661:     product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Движущийся остаток")

669:     )

681:         balance.quantity_unpacked = 7

698:     ]
699:     async with SessionLocal() as session:

703:                 InventoryBalance.product_id == product,
704:             )
705:         )

712:     assert delta == -3

812: @pytest.mark.asyncio
813: async def test_inventory_count_found_creates_line_and_second_scan_increments(
814:     async_client: AsyncClient,
815: ) -> None:
816:     """Находка: товар лежит в коробе, где по учёту его нет.
817: 
818:     Ради этого пересчёт и затевают. Первый скан заводит строку со счётом 1,
819:     второй прибавляет вторую штуку, а не плодит вторую строку.
820:     """
821:     setup = await _tenant(async_client, "FoundCount")
822:     counted = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Числится")
823:     surprise = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Находка")
824:     _pallet_id, box_id, _cargo_place_id, _empty_box_id = await _containers(setup)
825:     await _balance(setup, counted, 3, container_kind="box", container_id=box_id)
826: 
827:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
828:         product = await session.get(Product, surprise)
829:         assert product is not None
830:         product.wb_barcode = "4600000000001"
831:         await session.commit()
832: 
833:     created = await async_client.post(
834:         "/operations/inventory-counts",
835:         headers=setup.headers,
836:         json={"source": "object", "object": {"type": "box", "id": str(box_id)}},
837:     )
838:     assert created.status_code == 201, created.text
839:     count_id = created.json()["id"]
840: 
841:     body = {
842:         "barcodes": ["4600000000001"],
843:         "container_kind": "box",
844:         "container_id": str(box_id),
845:     }
846:     first = await async_client.post(
847:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count_id}/found", headers=setup.headers, json=body
848:     )
849:     assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
850:     found_line = next(
851:         line for line in first.json()["count"]["lines"] if line["product_id"] == str(surprise)
852:     )
853:     assert found_line["expected_quantity"] == 0
854:     assert found_line["actual_quantity"] == 1
855:     assert found_line["container_id"] == str(box_id)
856: 
857:     second = await async_client.post(
858:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count_id}/found", headers=setup.headers, json=body
859:     )
860:     assert second.status_code == 200, second.text
861:     surprise_lines = [
862:         line for line in second.json()["count"]["lines"] if line["product_id"] == str(surprise)
863:     ]
864:     assert len(surprise_lines) == 1
865:     assert surprise_lines[0]["actual_quantity"] == 2

989: @pytest.mark.asyncio
990: async def test_inventory_count_found_is_idempotent_per_scan(
991:     async_client: AsyncClient,
992: ) -> None:
993:     """Повтор того же скана не превращается в лишнюю штуку на остатке.
994: 
995:     Склад работает по вайфаю, который рвётся: ответ не доехал, экран показал
996:     ошибку, а запись уже прошла. Кладовщик сканирует ещё раз — и без этой
997:     защиты на остатке оказывается двойка, которую потом нечем найти.
998:     """
999:     setup = await _tenant(async_client, "IdemCount")
1000:     surprise = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Находка с повтором")
1001:     anchor = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Якорь")
1002:     await _balance(setup, anchor, 2)
1003:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
1004:         product = await session.get(Product, surprise)
1005:         assert product is not None
1006:         product.wb_barcode = "4600000000555"
1007:         await session.commit()
1008: 
1009:     created = await async_client.post(
1010:         "/operations/inventory-counts",
1011:         headers=setup.headers,
1012:         json={"source": "planned", "filters": {}},
1013:     )
1014:     assert created.status_code == 201, created.text
1015:     count_id = created.json()["id"]
1016: 
1017:     body = {
1018:         "barcodes": ["4600000000555"],
1019:         "cell_id": str(setup.location_id),
1020:         "scan_id": "scan-0001",
1021:     }
1022:     first = await async_client.post(
1023:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count_id}/found", headers=setup.headers, json=body
1024:     )
1025:     assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
1026: 
1027:     # Тот же скан ещё раз — как будто оператор не увидел ответа и повторил.
1028:     again = await async_client.post(
1029:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count_id}/found", headers=setup.headers, json=body
1030:     )
1031:     assert again.status_code == 200, again.text
1032: 
1033:     lines = [
1034:         line for line in again.json()["count"]["lines"] if line["product_id"] == str(surprise)
1035:     ]
1036:     assert len(lines) == 1
1037:     assert lines[0]["actual_quantity"] == 1
1038: 
1039:     # А настоящий второй пик — это уже другой скан, и он считается.
1040:     third = await async_client.post(
1041:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count_id}/found",
1042:         headers=setup.headers,
1043:         json={**body, "scan_id": "scan-0002"},
1044:     )
1045:     assert third.status_code == 200, third.text
1046:     line = next(
1047:         line for line in third.json()["count"]["lines"] if line["product_id"] == str(surprise)
1048:     )
1049:     assert line["actual_quantity"] == 2
1050: 
1051: 
1052: @pytest.mark.asyncio
1053: async def test_inventory_count_drops_empty_containers_but_keeps_them_scannable(
1054:     async_client: AsyncClient,
1055: ) -> None:
1056:     # Пустая по документу тара не должна занимать строку в дереве, но обязана
1057:     # открываться сканом. В пересчёте «Империи ФФ» из 420 коробов товар лежал в
1058:     # 113: остальные 307 висели строками «0 из 0», документ вырастал до сорока
1059:     # тысяч пикселей, и оператор не мог найти в нём свой короб глазами.
1060:     setup = await _tenant(async_client, "EmptyBox")
1061:     product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Товар в коробе")
1062:     _pallet_id, box_id, _cargo_place_id, empty_box_id = await _containers(setup)
1063:     await _balance(
1064:         setup,
1065:         product,
1066:         3,
1067:         location_id=setup.location_id,
1068:         container_kind="box",
1069:         container_id=box_id,
1070:     )
1071: 
1072:     count = await _create_all(async_client, setup)
1073: 
1074:     def container_ids(nodes: list[dict[str, object]]) -> set[str]:
1075:         found: set[str] = set()
1076:         for node in nodes:
1077:             if node["kind"] == "product":
1078:                 continue
1079:             found.add(str(node["id"]))
1080:             found |= container_ids(node["children"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]
1081:         return found
1082: 
1083:     in_tree: set[str] = set()
1084:     for cell in count["cells"]:
1085:         in_tree |= container_ids(cell["children"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]
1086: 
1087:     assert str(box_id) in in_tree
1088:     assert str(empty_box_id) not in in_tree
1089: 
1090:     scannable = {item["id"]: item for item in count["scannable_containers"]}
1091:     assert str(empty_box_id) in scannable
1092:     assert scannable[str(empty_box_id)]["kind"] == "box"
1093:     assert scannable[str(empty_box_id)]["cell_id"] == str(setup.location_id)
1094:     # Тара со строками остаётся в дереве и в списке сканируемой не дублируется.
1095:     assert str(box_id) not in scannable
1096:

1098: @pytest.mark.asyncio
1099: async def test_inventory_count_create_container_keeps_it_visible_but_not_other_empty_boxes(
1100:     async_client: AsyncClient,
1101: ) -> None:
1102:     # Кнопка «Создать короб» была мертва: тара создавалась на складе, но
1103:     # прунинг тут же выбрасывал её из дерева документа — она пуста по
1104:     # определению, оператор только что её завёл. Ручка
1105:     # POST /operations/inventory-counts/{id}/containers должна и создать
1106:     # тару, и удержать её в дереве, но не отключать прунинг для чужой пустой
1107:     # тары склада (см. test_inventory_count_drops_empty_containers_...).
1108:     setup = await _tenant(async_client, "NewBox")
1109:     product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Товар в коробе")
1110:     pallet_id, box_id, _cargo_place_id, empty_box_id = await _containers(setup)
1111:     await _balance(
1112:         setup,
1113:         product,
1114:         3,
1115:         location_id=setup.location_id,
1116:         container_kind="box",
1117:         container_id=box_id,
1118:     )
1119: 
1120:     count = await _create_all(async_client, setup)
1121: 
1122:     response = await async_client.post(
1123:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}/containers",
1124:         headers=setup.headers,
1125:         json={"kind": "box"},
1126:     )
1127:     assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
1128:     body = response.json()
1129: 
1130:     def container_ids(nodes: list[dict[str, object]]) -> set[str]:
1131:         found: set[str] = set()
1132:         for node in nodes:
1133:             if node["kind"] == "product":
1134:                 continue
1135:             found.add(str(node["id"]))
1136:             found |= container_ids(node["children"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]
1137:         return found
1138: 
1139:     in_tree: set[str] = set()
1140:     for cell in body["cells"]:
1141:         in_tree |= container_ids(cell["children"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]
1142: 
1143:     # Новый короб виден в дереве документа сразу после создания. Короб с
1144:     # товаром и его палета (родитель, у которого теперь есть непустой
1145:     # ребёнок) остаются в дереве по обычному правилу «не пусто — не трогаем».
1146:     created_ids = in_tree - {str(box_id), str(pallet_id)}
1147:     assert len(created_ids) == 1, in_tree
1148:     created_id = next(iter(created_ids))
1149:     assert created_id != str(empty_box_id)
1150: 
1151:     # ...а старая пустая тара склада, не заведённая через эту ручку,
1152:     # по-прежнему выброшена из дерева: общее правило прунинга не отключилось.
1153:     assert str(empty_box_id) not in in_tree
1154:     scannable_ids = {item["id"] for item in body["scannable_containers"]}
1155:     assert str(empty_box_id) in scannable_ids
1156:     assert created_id not in scannable_ids
1157: 
1158:     # Исключение привязано к документу, а не только к разовому ответу ручки:
1159:     # обычное переоткрытие документа отдаёт тот же результат.
1160:     reopened = await async_client.get(
1161:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}", headers=setup.headers
1162:     )
1163:     assert reopened.status_code == 200, reopened.text
1164:     reopened_tree: set[str] = set()
1165:     for cell in reopened.json()["cells"]:
1166:         reopened_tree |= container_ids(cell["children"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]
1167:     assert created_id in reopened_tree
1168:     assert str(empty_box_id) not in reopened_tree
1169:

1171: @pytest.mark.asyncio
1172: @pytest.mark.parametrize("kind", ["box", "cargo_place", "pallet"])
1173: async def test_inventory_count_container_and_document_link_commit_together(
1174:     async_client: AsyncClient, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, kind: str,
1175: ) -> None:
1176:     """WMS-375/A29: retry after a failed link must not leave an extra container."""
1177:     setup = await _tenant(async_client, "AtomicContainer")
1178:     product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Synthetic count product")
1179:     await _balance(setup, product, 3)
1180:     count = await _create_all(async_client, setup)
1181:     url = f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}/containers"
1182:     real_create = inventory_count_service.warehouse_map_service.create_sorting_object
1183: 
1184:     async def fail_before_document_link(*args, **kwargs):
1185:         await real_create(*args, **kwargs)
1186:         raise RuntimeError("injected failure before document link")
1187: 
1188:     monkeypatch.setattr(
1189:         inventory_count_service.warehouse_map_service,
1190:         "create_sorting_object", fail_before_document_link,
1191:     )
1192:     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="injected failure"):
1193:         await async_client.post(url, headers=setup.headers, json={"kind": kind})
1194:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
1195:         assert await session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(WarehouseBox)) == 0
1196:         assert await session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(Pallet)) == 0
1197:         assert await session.scalar(
1198:             select(func.count()).select_from(InventoryCountCreatedContainer)
1199:         ) == 0
1200:         assert await session.scalar(select(func.sum(InventoryBalance.quantity))) == 3
1201:         assert await session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(InventoryMovement)) == 0
1202: 
1203:     monkeypatch.setattr(
1204:         inventory_count_service.warehouse_map_service, "create_sorting_object", real_create,
1205:     )
1206:     retry = await async_client.post(url, headers=setup.headers, json={"kind": kind})
1207:     assert retry.status_code == 200, retry.text
1208:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
1209:         boxes = await session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(WarehouseBox))
1210:         pallets = await session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(Pallet))
1211:         assert (boxes, pallets) == ((0, 1) if kind == "pallet" else (1, 0))
1212:         links = list((await session.scalars(select(InventoryCountCreatedContainer))).all())
1213:         assert len(links) == 1
1214:         assert links[0].count_id == uuid.UUID(str(count["id"]))
1215:         assert links[0].container_kind == kind
1216:         model = Pallet if kind == "pallet" else WarehouseBox
1217:         assert await session.get(model, links[0].container_id) is not None

1253: @pytest.mark.asyncio
1254: async def test_inventory_count_records_found_into_container_dropped_as_empty(
1255:     async_client: AsyncClient,
1256: ) -> None:
1257:     # Пустой короб выброшен из дерева, но оператор подошёл к нему и нашёл товар.
1258:     # Находка обязана лечь именно в этот короб — иначе выброс строки превратился
1259:     # бы в запрет считать то, ради чего пересчёт и делают.

1261:     product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Товар в коробе")
1262:     found_product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Найденный товар")
1263:     _pallet_id, box_id, _cargo_place_id, empty_box_id = await _containers(setup)
1264:     await _balance(
1265:         setup,
1266:         product,
1267:         3,
1268:         location_id=setup.location_id,
1269:         container_kind="box",
1270:         container_id=box_id,
1271:     )
1272:     barcode = f"FOUND-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]}"
1273:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
1274:         card = await session.get(Product, found_product)
1275:         assert card is not None
1276:         card.wb_barcode = barcode
1277:         await session.commit()
1278: 
1279:     count = await _create_all(async_client, setup)
1280:     assert str(empty_box_id) in {item["id"] for item in count["scannable_containers"]}
1281: 
1282:     found = await async_client.post(
1283:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}/found",
1284:         headers=setup.headers,
1285:         json={
1286:             "barcodes": [barcode],
1287:             "container_kind": "box",
1288:             "container_id": str(empty_box_id),
1289:             "scan_id": str(uuid.uuid4()),
1290:         },
1291:     )
1292:     assert found.status_code == 200, found.text
1293:     line = next(
1294:         item
1295:         for item in found.json()["count"]["lines"]
1296:         if item["product_id"] == str(found_product)
1297:     )
1298:     assert line["container_id"] == str(empty_box_id)
1299:     assert line["actual_quantity"] == 1

1302: @pytest.mark.asyncio
1303: async def test_inventory_count_reports_ordinary_and_fbs_deductions(
1304:     async_client: AsyncClient,
1305: ) -> None:
1306:     from app.models.fbs_binding_stock_pool import FbsBindingStockPool
1307:     from app.models.fbs_warehouse_binding import FbsWarehouseBinding
1308: 
1309:     setup = await _tenant(async_client, "FbsBreakdown")
1310:     seller_id = await _seller(async_client, setup, "ФБС селлер")
1311:     product_id = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Футболка", seller_id=seller_id)
1312:     await _balance(setup, product_id, 400)
1313:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
1314:         product = await session.get(Product, product_id)
1315:         assert product is not None
1316:         product.fbs_units_mode = True
1317:         for warehouse_number, quantity in [(501001, 100), (501002, 200)]:
1318:             binding = FbsWarehouseBinding(
1319:                 tenant_id=setup.tenant_id, seller_id=seller_id,
1320:                 wms_warehouse_id=setup.warehouse_id,
1321:                 wb_warehouse_id=warehouse_number, marketplace="wb",
1322:             )
1323:             session.add(binding)
1324:             await session.flush()
1325:             session.add(FbsBindingStockPool(
1326:                 tenant_id=setup.tenant_id, binding_id=binding.id,
1327:                 product_id=product_id, quantity=quantity,
1328:             ))
1329:         await session.commit()
1330:     count = await _create_all(async_client, setup)
1331:     saved = await async_client.put(
1332:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}/lines",
1333:         headers=setup.headers,
1334:         json={"lines": [{"line_id": count["lines"][0]["id"], "actual_quantity": 250}]},
1335:     )
1336:     assert saved.status_code == 200, saved.text
1337:     posted = await async_client.post(
1338:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}/post", headers=setup.headers,
1339:     )
1340:     assert posted.status_code == 200, posted.text
1341:     summary = await async_client.get(
1342:         "/operations/inventory-balances/summary", headers=setup.headers
1343:     )
1344:     assert summary.status_code == 200, summary.text
1345:     stock = next(row for row in summary.json() if row["product_id"] == str(product_id))
1346:     assert stock["quantity_fbs"] == 250
1347:     assert stock["quantity_free_fbo"] == 0
1348:     assert stock["available"] == 0
1349:     deductions = posted.json()["stock_write_off"]
1350:     assert sum(row["quantity"] for row in deductions if row["marketplace"] is None) == 100
1351:     fbs = [row for row in deductions if row["marketplace"] == "wb"]
1352:     assert sum(row["quantity"] for row in fbs) == 50
1353:     assert all(row["warehouse_id"] in {"501001", "501002"} for row in fbs)
1354:     assert all(row["product_id"] == str(product_id) for row in deductions)
1355:     repeat = await async_client.post(
1356:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}/post", headers=setup.headers,
1357:     )
1358:     assert repeat.status_code == 409
1359:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
1360:         assert await session.scalar(select(func.sum(InventoryBalance.quantity)).where(
1361:             InventoryBalance.product_id == product_id,
1362:         )) == 250
1363:         assert await session.scalar(select(func.sum(FbsBindingStockPool.quantity)).where(
1364:             FbsBindingStockPool.product_id == product_id,
1365:         )) == 250

1372:     # ищет по каталогу и вводит количество сразу, а не сканирует по штуке.

1387:     )

1394:     )

1396:     assert line["expected_quantity"] == 0
1397:     assert line["container_kind"] is None
1398:     assert line["container_id"] is None
1399: 
1400:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
1401:         sorting = await get_or_create_sorting_location(
1402:             session, setup.tenant_id, setup.warehouse_id
1403:         )
1404:         db_line = await session.scalar(
1405:             select(InventoryCountLine).where(InventoryCountLine.product_id == product)
1406:         )
1407:         assert db_line is not None
1408:         assert db_line.storage_location_id == sorting.id

1477: @pytest.mark.asyncio
1478: async def test_inventory_count_manual_line_merges_into_existing_line_at_same_place(
1479:     async_client: AsyncClient,
1480: ) -> None:
1481:     # Тот же товар в то же место добавляют второй раз (например, нашли ещё) —
1482:     # прибавляем к уже насчитанному, а не заводим вторую строку поверх первой:
1483:     # уникальный индекс документа (product+location+container) этого и не
1484:     # позволил бы, а прибавление — тот же ответ, что и у повторного скана.
1485:     setup = await _tenant(async_client, "ManualMerge")
1486:     product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Товар дважды руками")
1487:     count = await _create_all(async_client, setup)
1488: 
1489:     first = await async_client.post(
1490:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}/manual-line",
1491:         headers=setup.headers,

1602: # оператором ячейку — а не «на складе без адреса», как раньше. Сама механика

1798: 
1799: def _container_ids_in_tree(body: dict[str, object]) -> set[str]:
1800:     def walk(nodes: list[dict[str, object]]) -> set[str]:
1801:         found: set[str] = set()
1802:         for node in nodes:
1803:             if node["kind"] == "product":
1804:                 continue
1805:             found.add(str(node["id"]))
1806:             found |= walk(node["children"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]
1807:         return found
1808: 
1809:     out: set[str] = set()
1810:     for cell in body["cells"]:  # type: ignore[union-attr]
1811:         out |= walk(cell["children"])  # type: ignore[arg-type,index]
1812:     return out
1813: 
1814: 
1815: async def _create_container(
1816:     async_client: AsyncClient,
1817:     setup: TenantSetup,
1818:     count_id: str,
1819:     kind: str,
1820:     *,
1821:     cell_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,
1822: ) -> uuid.UUID:
1823:     payload: dict[str, object] = {"kind": kind}
1824:     if cell_id is not None:
1825:         payload["cell_id"] = str(cell_id)
1826:     response = await async_client.post(
1827:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count_id}/containers",
1828:         headers=setup.headers,
1829:         json=payload,
1830:     )
1831:     assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
1832:     created = _container_ids_in_tree(response.json())
1833:     assert len(created) == 1, created
1834:     return uuid.UUID(next(iter(created)))
1835:

1837: @pytest.mark.asyncio
1838: async def test_inventory_count_move_line_transfers_balance_and_line_address(
1839:     async_client: AsyncClient,
1840: ) -> None:
1841:     # Задача 2 (WMS-153): «Переложить товар в тару» переносит и физический
1842:     # остаток (тот же механизм, что и на карте склада), и адрес строки
1843:     # документа — иначе после переноса строка показывала бы недостачу там,
1844:     # откуда товар уже забрали, хотя оператор его просто переложил.
1845:     setup = await _tenant(async_client, "MoveLine")
1846:     product = await _product(async_client, setup, name="Товар для переноса")
1847:     await _balance(setup, product, 4)
1848:     count = await _create_all(async_client, setup)
1849:     # Короб заводим в той же ячейке, что и остаток товара, — реалистичный
1850:     # случай (задача 1): оператор стоит у полки, короб появляется тут же.
1851:     box_id = await _create_container(
1852:         async_client, setup, count["id"], "box", cell_id=setup.location_id
1853:     )
1854:     line = next(item for item in count["lines"] if item["product_id"] == str(product))
1855:     assert line["container_kind"] is None
1856:     assert line["storage_location_id"] == str(setup.location_id)
1857: 
1858:     response = await async_client.post(
1859:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}/lines/{line['id']}/move",
1860:         headers=setup.headers,
1861:         json={"container_kind": "box", "container_id": str(box_id)},
1862:     )
1863:     assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
1864:     moved = next(
1865:         item for item in response.json()["lines"] if item["product_id"] == str(product)
1866:     )
1867:     assert moved["id"] == line["id"]
1868:     assert moved["container_kind"] == "box"
1869:     assert moved["container_id"] == str(box_id)
1870:     assert moved["storage_location_id"] == str(setup.location_id)
1871: 
1872:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
1873:         loose = await session.scalar(
1874:             select(InventoryBalance).where(
1875:                 InventoryBalance.product_id == product,
1876:                 InventoryBalance.storage_location_id == setup.location_id,
1877:                 InventoryBalance.container_id.is_(None),
1878:             )
1879:         )
1880:         assert loose is None or loose.quantity == 0
1881:         in_box = await session.scalar(
1882:             select(InventoryBalance).where(
1883:                 InventoryBalance.product_id == product,
1884:                 InventoryBalance.container_kind == "box",
1885:                 InventoryBalance.container_id == box_id,
1886:             )
1887:         )
1888:         assert in_box is not None
1889:         assert in_box.quantity == 4
1890:

1963:     assert added.status_code == 200, added.text

1965:         item
1966:         for item in added.json()["count"]["lines"]
1967:         if item["product_id"] == str(product)
1968:     )
1969:

1971:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count['id']}/lines/{line['id']}/move",
1972:         headers=setup.headers,
1973:         json={"container_kind": "box", "container_id": str(box_id)},
1974:     )
1975:     assert response.status_code == 409
1976:     assert response.json()["detail"] == "move_source_empty"

2053: @pytest.mark.asyncio
2054: async def test_inventory_count_delete_container_rejects_non_empty(
2055:     async_client: AsyncClient,
2056: ) -> None:
2057:     # Ручки удаления не было вовсе, и главное требование постановки —
2058:     # удалять можно только по-настоящему пустую тару, иначе отказ понятным
2059:     # сообщением. Проверяем по живому остатку, а не по строкам этого
2060:     # документа: документ может быть сужен по селлеру/категории, и «пусто в
2061:     # документе» — не то же самое, что «пусто физически».
2062:     setup = await _tenant(async_client, "DeleteNonEmpty")
2063:     seller_id = await _seller(async_client, setup, "Другой селлер")
2064:     other_product = await _product(
2065:         async_client, setup, name="Чужой товар", seller_id=seller_id
2066:     )
2067:     count_for_all = await _create_all(async_client, setup)
2068:     box_id = await _create_container(async_client, setup, count_for_all["id"], "box")
2069:     # Остаток кладём ПОСЛЕ наполнения документа и от другого селлера — у
2070:     # документа этого селлера в строках нет, но физически короб не пуст.
2071:     await _balance(
2072:         setup,
2073:         other_product,
2074:         5,
2075:         location_id=setup.location_id,
2076:         container_kind="box",
2077:         container_id=box_id,
2078:     )
2079: 
2080:     response = await async_client.delete(
2081:         f"/operations/inventory-counts/{count_for_all['id']}/containers/box/{box_id}",
2082:         headers=setup.headers,
2083:     )
2084:     assert response.status_code == 409
2085:     assert response.json()["detail"] == "container_not_empty"
2086: 
2087:     async with SessionLocal() as session:
2088:         assert await session.get(WarehouseBox, box_id) is not None

63: // Русские числительные: «1 движение», «2 движения», «5 движений». Без трёх форм
64: // экран пишет «создаст 4 движений» и читается как машинный перевод.
65: function plural(n: number, one: string, few: string, many: string): string {
66:   const mod10 = n % 10
67:   const mod100 = n % 100
68:   if (mod100 >= 11 && mod100 <= 14) return `${n} ${many}`
69:   if (mod10 === 1) return `${n} ${one}`
70:   if (mod10 >= 2 && mod10 <= 4) return `${n} ${few}`
71:   return `${n} ${many}`
72: }
73: 
74: const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
75: 
76: // Товар в документе можно добавить в несколько мест (см. record_found): не
77: // прячем от повторного выбора ни один товар, даже если строка по нему уже есть.
78: const NO_DISABLED_PRODUCTS = new Set<string>()
79: 
80: type ManualAddPlacement = {
81:   cellId: string | null
82:   containerKind: 'pallet' | 'box' | 'cargo_place' | null
83:   containerId: string | null
84: }
85: 
86: /**
87:  * Куда положить товар, добавленный руками, — из строки, на которой стоит
88:  * выделение (задача 2). Постановка прямо описывает только короб (п. 6) и
89:  * «выделения не было» (п. 7); ячейку ведём тем же путём, что и находка со
90:  * сканера с открытой ячейкой без тары — иначе выбрать ячейку в задаче 2 было
91:  * бы нечем воспользоваться в задаче 3.
92:  */
93: export function selectionPlacement(row: InvRow | null): ManualAddPlacement {
94:   if (!row || row.kind === 'product') return { cellId: null, containerKind: null, containerId: null }
95:   if (row.kind === 'cell') {
96:     // Служебные строки вида «Без ячеек» — не настоящий адрес склада, для них
97:     // ведём себя так же, как без выделения: находка уйдёт в зону сортировки.
98:     return { cellId: UUID_RE.test(row.id) ? row.id : null, containerKind: null, containerId: null }
99:   }
100:   return { cellId: null, containerKind: row.kind, containerId: row.id }
101: }
102: 
103: /**
104:  * Тара, на которой стоит выделение, — для двух новых действий (задачи 2 и 3
105:  * доработки от 03.09.2026, WMS-153): «Переложить сюда» и «Удалить тару».
106:  * Обе умеют работать только с тарой, а не с ячейкой — переносить умеем
107:  * только в тару, а удалять умеем только тару.
108:  */
109: export function selectedContainer(
110:   row: InvRow | null,
111: ): { kind: 'pallet' | 'box' | 'cargo_place'; id: string } | null {
112:   if (!row || row.kind === 'product' || row.kind === 'cell') return null
113:   return { kind: row.kind, id: row.id }
114: }
