1412:   const addLineByBarcode = async (rawInput?: string) => {
1413:     if (!detail) return
1414:     const code = (rawInput ?? '').trim()
1415:     if (!code) return
1416:     setBusy(true)
1417:     setError(null)
1418:     try {

1420:       if (cat == null) {
1421:         cat = await fetchCatalogRows()
1422:         setCatalog(cat)
1423:       }
1424:       const productId = resolveProductIdByBarcode(cat, code)
1425:       if (!productId) {
1426:         setPickerInitialSearch(code)
1427:         setError('Товар не найден в каталоге селлера. Добавление нового товара будет отдельной задачей.')
1428:         return
1429:       }

1431:       if (existing) {
1432:         const res = await fetch(
1433:           apiUrl(
1434:             `/operations/inbound-intake-requests/${requestId}/lines/${existing.id}/expected`,
1435:           ),
1436:           {
1437:             method: 'PATCH',
1438:             headers: { ...authHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
1439:             body: JSON.stringify({ expected_qty: existing.expected_qty + 1 }),
1440:           },
1441:         )
1442:         if (!res.ok) {
1443:           setError(await readApiErrorMessage(res))
1444:           return
1445:         }
1446:       } else {
1447:         const res = await fetch(apiUrl(`/operations/inbound-intake-requests/${requestId}/lines`), {
1448:           method: 'POST',
1449:           headers: { ...authHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
1450:           body: JSON.stringify({ product_id: productId, expected_qty: 1 }),
1451:         })
1452:         if (!res.ok) {
1453:           setError(await readApiErrorMessage(res))
1454:           return
1455:         }
1456:       }
1457:       await loadDetail()
1458:     } catch (e) {
1459:       setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Не удалось добавить строку по штрихкоду.')
1460:     } finally {
1461:       setBusy(false)
1462:     }
1463:   }

1465:   const applyPicker = async (pickerQtyByProduct: Record<string, number>) => {
1466:     if (!detail) return
1467:     setBusy(true)
1468:     setError(null)
1469:     try {
1470:       const lineByProduct = new Map(detail.lines.map((ln) => [ln.product_id, ln]))
1471:       const receivingMode = detail.status !== 'draft' && receivingActive
1472:       for (const [productId, rawQty] of Object.entries(pickerQtyByProduct)) {
1473:         const addQty = Number.isFinite(rawQty) ? Math.floor(rawQty) : 0
1474:         if (addQty <= 0) continue
1475:         if (receivingMode) {
1476:           const added = await addReceivedProductFact(productId, addQty)
1477:           if (!added) {
1478:             return
1479:           }
1480:           continue
1481:         }
1482:         const existing = lineByProduct.get(productId)
1483:         if (existing) {
1484:           const next = existing.expected_qty + addQty
1485:           const res = await fetch(
1486:             apiUrl(
1487:               `/operations/inbound-intake-requests/${requestId}/lines/${existing.id}/expected`,
1488:             ),
1489:             {
1490:               method: 'PATCH',
1491:               headers: { ...authHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
1492:               body: JSON.stringify({ expected_qty: next }),
1493:             },
1494:           )
1495:           if (!res.ok) {
1496:             setError(await readApiErrorMessage(res))
1497:             return
1498:           }
1499:         } else {
1500:           const res = await fetch(
1501:             apiUrl(`/operations/inbound-intake-requests/${requestId}/lines`),
1502:             {
1503:               method: 'POST',
1504:               headers: { ...authHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
1505:               body: JSON.stringify({ product_id: productId, expected_qty: addQty }),
1506:             },
1507:           )
1508:           if (!res.ok) {
1509:             setError(await readApiErrorMessage(res))
1510:             return
1511:           }
1512:         }
1513:       }
1514:       setPickerOpen(false)
1515:       await loadDetail()
1516:     } catch (e) {
1517:       setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Не удалось добавить товары.')
1518:     } finally {
1519:       setBusy(false)
1520:     }
1521:   }

1587:   const printInboundInternalLabels = async (
1588:     targets: InboundInternalLabelTarget[],
1589:     labelSize: LabelSize,
1590:   ) => {
1591:     if (targets.length === 0) return
1592:     setBusy(true)
1593:     setError(null)
1594:     try {
1595:       // Один iframe = одно задание принтеру. Иначе «Печать коробов» открывает
1596:       // диалог принтера для каждого короба и рвёт непрерывную ленту.
1597:       printBarcodeLabels(targets.map((target) => ({
1598:         title: target.kind === 'box' ? `Короб № ${target.number}` : `Грузоместо № ${target.number}`,
1599:         barcode: target.barcode,
1600:         barcodeDataUrl: renderBarcodeDataUrl(target.barcode, { variant: 'internalBox' }),
1601:         labelSize,
1602:         layout: 'internalBox' as const,
1603:       })))
1604:       for (const target of targets) {
1605:         const path = target.kind === 'box'
1606:           ? `/operations/inbound-intake-requests/${requestId}/boxes/${target.id}/mark-label-printed`
1607:           : `/operations/inbound-intake-requests/${requestId}/cargo-places/${target.id}/mark-label-printed`
1608:         const res = await fetch(apiUrl(path), { method: 'POST', headers: authHeaders })
1609:         if (!res.ok) {
1610:           throw new Error(await readApiErrorMessage(res))
1611:         }
1612:       }
1613:       await loadDetail()
1614:     } catch (e) {
1615:       setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Не удалось напечатать этикетки.')
1616:     } finally {
1617:       setBusy(false)
1618:     }
1619:   }

1677:   const confirmInboundBoxPrint = async (labelSize: LabelSize) => {
1678:     const target = boxPrintTarget
1679:     setBoxPrintTarget(null)
1680:     if (!target) return
1681:     if (target.kind === 'box') {
1682:       await printInboundBoxLabel(target.box, labelSize)
1683:     } else if (target.kind === 'box-all') {
1684:       await printAllInboundBoxLabels(labelSize)
1685:     } else if (target.kind === 'cargo') {
1686:       await printInboundCargoPlaceLabel(target.place, labelSize)
1687:     } else {
1688:       await printAllInboundCargoPlaceLabels(labelSize)
1689:     }
1690:   }

1692:   const scanToReceiving = async (raw?: string) => {
1693:     const code = (raw ?? '').trim()
1694:     if (!code || scanDocument.current !== requestId) return
1695:     setError(null)
1696:     setScanAddBarcode(null)
1697:     try {
1698:       if (isInboundMarkingScan(code)) {
1699:         await marking.attach(code, lastProductScan.current)
1700:         return
1701:       }
1702:       lastProductScan.current = null
1703:       receivingScanReconciler.cancel()
1704:       ++loadDetailSeq.current
1705:       const productId = findInboundScanProductId(code, scanProductByBarcode)
1706:       const res = await fetch(
1707:         apiUrl(`/operations/inbound-intake-requests/${requestId}/receiving/scan`),
1708:         {
1709:           method: 'POST',
1710:           headers: { ...authHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
1711:           body: JSON.stringify({ barcode: code, product_id: productId }),
1712:         },
1713:       )
1714:       if (!res.ok) {
1715:         const errorCode = await readApiErrorMessage(res)
1716:         setScanToastError(scanErrorMessageRu(errorCode))
1717:         setScanAddBarcode(
1718:           errorCode === 'product_not_on_request' || errorCode === 'barcode_unknown'
1719:             ? code
1720:             : null,
1721:         )
1722:         return
1723:       }
1724:       setScanAddBarcode(null)
1725:       const scannedLine = (await res.json()) as InboundLine
1726:       if (scanDocument.current !== requestId) return
1727:       ++loadDetailSeq.current
1728:       lastProductScan.current = scannedLine.id
1729:       setDetail((current) => {
1730:         if (!current) return current
1731:         return {
1732:           ...current,
1733:           lines: applyScannedInboundLine(current.lines, scannedLine),
1734:         }
1735:       })
1736:       setLastScannedLineId(scannedLine.id)
1737:       if (isReturnOperation && returnAutoPrint) {
1738:         printReturnBarcodeForLine(scannedLine)
1739:       }
1740:       // Coalesce the expensive document reload until the operator pauses scanning.
1741:       // The POST response already contains the authoritative changed line.
1742:       receivingScanReconciler.schedule()
1743:     } catch (e) {
1744:       setScanToastError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Не удалось выполнить скан.')
1745:     }
1746:   }

1871:           headers: { ...authHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },

1873:             pallet_id: combinePalletId || null,
1874:             inbound_request_id: requestId,
1875:             inbound_box_ids: [...selectedPalletBoxIds],
1876:             cargo_place_ids: [],
1877:             warehouse_box_ids: [],
1878:           }),
1879:         },
1880:       )
1881:       if (!res.ok) {
1882:         setError(await readApiErrorMessage(res))
1883:         return
1884:       }
1885:       setCombinePalletOpen(false)
1886:       setSelectedPalletBoxIds(new Set())
1887:       await loadDetail()
1888:     } catch (e) {
1889:       setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Не удалось собрать палету.')
1890:     } finally {
1891:       setCombinePalletBusy(false)
1892:     }
1893:   }
1894: 
1895:   const setBoxDamaged = async (boxId: string, isDamaged: boolean) => {
1896:     setBusy(true)
1897:     try {
1898:       const response = await fetch(apiUrl(`/operations/inbound-intake-requests/${requestId}/boxes/${boxId}/damaged`), {
1899:         method: 'PATCH',
1900:         headers: { ...authHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
1901:         body: JSON.stringify({ is_damaged: isDamaged }),
1902:       })
1903:       if (!response.ok) throw new Error(await readApiErrorMessage(response))
1904:       const updated = await response.json() as InboundBox
1905:       setDetail((current) => current?.id === requestId
1906:         ? { ...current, boxes: current.boxes.map((box) => box.id === updated.id ? updated : box) }
1907:         : current)
1908:     } catch (error) {
1909:       setError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Не удалось сохранить отметку')
1910:     } finally {
1911:       setBusy(false)
1912:     }
1913:   }

1915:   const deleteInboundBox = async (boxId: string) => {
1916:     setBusy(true)
1917:     setError(null)
1918:     try {
1919:       const res = await fetch(
1920:         apiUrl(`/operations/inbound-intake-requests/${requestId}/boxes/${boxId}`),
1921:         { method: 'DELETE', headers: authHeaders },
1922:       )
1923:       if (!res.ok) {
1924:         setError(scanErrorMessageRu(await readApiErrorMessage(res)))
1925:         return
1926:       }
1927:       if (boxAddDialogBoxId === boxId) {
1928:         setBoxAddDialogBoxId(null)
1929:       }
1930:       await loadDetail()
1931:     } catch (e) {
1932:       setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Не удалось удалить короб.')
1933:     } finally {
1934:       setBusy(false)
1935:     }
1936:   }

1938:   const completeReceiving = async () => {
1939:     setBusy(true)
1940:     setError(null)
1941:     setFinishConfirmOpen(false)
1942:     try {
1943:       await receivingScanQueue(async () => undefined)
1944:       receivingScanReconciler.cancel()
1945:       ++loadDetailSeq.current
1946:       const res = await fetch(
1947:         apiUrl(`/operations/inbound-intake-requests/${requestId}/complete-receiving`),
1948:         { method: 'POST', headers: authHeaders },
1949:       )
1950:       if (!res.ok) {
1951:         setError(scanErrorMessageRu(await readApiErrorMessage(res)))
1952:         return
1953:       }
1954:       const completed = (await res.json()) as InboundDetail
1955:       ++loadDetailSeq.current
1956:       setDetail(completed)
1957:       setDistOpen(true)
1958:     } catch (e) {
1959:       setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Не удалось завершить приёмку.')
1960:     } finally {
1961:       setBusy(false)
1962:     }
1963:   }

1967:     setError(null)
1968:     try {
1969:       const res = await fetch(
1970:         apiUrl(`/operations/inbound-intake-requests/${requestId}/reopen-receiving`),
1971:         { method: 'POST', headers: authHeaders },
1972:       )
1973:       if (!res.ok) {
1974:         setError(await readApiErrorMessage(res))
1975:         return
1976:       }
1977:       setDistOpen(false)
1978:       await loadDetail()
1979:     } catch (e) {
1980:       setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Не удалось открыть приёмку для редактирования.')
1981:     } finally {
1982:       setBusy(false)
1983:     }
1984:   }

1988:       setFinishConfirmOpen(true)
1989:       return
1990:     }
1991:     void completeReceiving()
1992:   }

1994:   const setLineActual = async (lineId: string, actualQty: number): Promise<boolean> => {
1995:     setBusy(true)
1996:     setError(null)
1997:     try {
1998:       const res = await fetch(
1999:         apiUrl(`/operations/inbound-intake-requests/${requestId}/lines/${lineId}/actual`),
2000:         {
2001:           method: 'PATCH',
2002:           headers: { ...authHeaders, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
2003:           body: JSON.stringify({ actual_qty: actualQty }),
2004:         },
2005:       )
2006:       if (!res.ok) {
2007:         const msg = await readApiErrorMessage(res)
2008:         setError(msg === 'actual_missing' ? 'Укажите факт по всем строкам.' : msg)
2009:         return false
2010:       }
2011:       await loadDetail()
2012:       return true
2013:     } catch (e) {
2014:       setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Не удалось сохранить факт.')
2015:       return false
2016:     } finally {
2017:       setBusy(false)
2018:     }
2019:   }

2021:   const saveManualLineActual = async (lineId: string, rawOverride?: string) => {
2022:     const raw =
2023:       rawOverride ??
2024:       actualDraftRef.current[lineId] ??
2025:       actualDraftByLineId[lineId]
2026:     const validationError = integerQtyError(raw)
2027:     if (validationError) {
2028:       setActualDraftErrorByLineId((prev) => ({ ...prev, [lineId]: validationError }))
2029:       setManualEditLineId(lineId)
2030:       focusActualInput(lineId)
2031:       return
2032:     }
2033:     const displayed = parseIntegerQty(raw)
2034:     if (displayed == null) {
2035:       return
2036:     }
2037:     const line = detail?.lines.find((ln) => ln.id === lineId)
2038:     if (!line) {
2039:       return
2040:     }
2041:     const boxes = detail?.boxes ?? []
2042:     const cargoPlaces = detail?.cargo_places ?? []
2043:     const currentEffective = effectiveActualQty(line, boxes, detail?.status)
2044:     if (displayed === currentEffective) {
2045:       setActualDraftErrorByLineId((prev) => ({ ...prev, [lineId]: '' }))
2046:       setManualEditLineId(null)
2047:       return
2048:     }
2049:     const loose = looseQtyFromDisplayedTotal(displayed, line, boxes, cargoPlaces)
2050:     if (loose == null) {
2051:       const containerTotal = containerTotalForProduct(boxes, cargoPlaces, line.product_id)
2052:       setActualDraftErrorByLineId((prev) => ({
2053:         ...prev,
2054:         [lineId]: `Нельзя указать меньше ${containerTotal}: товар уже разложен по коробам и грузоместам.`,
2055:       }))
2056:       setManualEditLineId(lineId)
2057:       focusActualInput(lineId)
2058:       return
2059:     }
2060:     const saved = await setLineActual(lineId, loose)
2061:     if (!saved) {
2062:       setManualEditLineId(lineId)
2063:       focusActualInput(lineId)
2064:       return
2065:     }
2066:     setActualDraftErrorByLineId((prev) => ({ ...prev, [lineId]: '' }))
2067:     setManualEditLineId(null)
2068:   }
2069: 
2070:   const hasUnsavedActualChange = useMemo(() => {
2071:     if (!manualEditLineId || !detail) return false
2072:     const line = detail.lines.find((ln) => ln.id === manualEditLineId)
2073:     if (!line) return false
2074:     const raw = actualDraftByLineId[manualEditLineId]
2075:     if (raw == null) return false
2076:     const current = String(effectiveActualQty(line, detail.boxes ?? [], detail.status))
2077:     return raw.trim() !== current
2078:   }, [actualDraftByLineId, detail, manualEditLineId])

4036:             variant="contained"
4037:             color="warning"
4038:             disabled={busy}
4039:             onClick={() => void completeReceiving()}
4040:             data-testid="ff-inbound-discrepancy-confirm"
4041:           >
4042:             Завершить приёмку
4043:           </Button>
4044:         </DialogActions>
4045:       </Dialog>
4046:       <BoxLabelPrintDialog
4047:         open={boxPrintTarget !== null}
4048:         title={
4049:           boxPrintTarget?.kind === 'cargo' || boxPrintTarget?.kind === 'cargo-all'
4050:             ? 'Печать этикетки грузоместа'
4051:             : 'Печать этикетки короба'
4052:         }
4053:         busy={busy}
4054:         onClose={() => setBoxPrintTarget(null)}
4055:         onConfirm={(size) => void confirmInboundBoxPrint(size)}
4056:         testId="ff-inbound-box-print-dialog"
4057:       />
4058:     </Box>
4059:     </FfProductMarkingPrintProvider>
4060:   )
4061: }
